`Cargo.toml`:

```toml
[package]
name = "bevy_input_driver"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2024"
description = "Drive a running Bevy game with injected input and screenshots over BRP or a script file."
license = "MIT OR Apache-2.0"
repository = "https://github.com/halfdeadgames/bevy_input_driver"
readme = "README.md"
keywords = ["bevy", "testing", "automation", "input", "screenshot"]
categories = ["game-development", "development-tools::testing"]
publish = false

[dependencies]
bevy = { version = "0.20.1", default-features = false, features = [
    "std",
    "bevy_log",
    "bevy_window",
    "bevy_render",
    "bevy_remote",
    "serialize",
    "png",
] }
serde = { version = "1", features = ["derive"] }
serde_json = "1"

[dev-dependencies]
bevy = "0.20.1"

[profile.dev]
opt-level = 1

[profile.dev.package."*"]
opt-level = 3

`README.md`:

```md
# bevy_input_driver

Lets an automated tester drive a running Bevy 0.20 game. It injects keyboard, mouse (including drags), scroll, touch, and gesture input, and takes screenshots. Commands arrive live over the Bevy Remote Protocol (BRP, HTTP JSON-RPC on 127.0.0.1) or from a JSON Lines file named at launch. The game cannot tell injected input from real input: each input is written as both its `bevy_input` message and the matching `WindowEvent`, the same way `bevy_winit` does, so `bevy_picking` and `bevy_ui` react normally. By default the real OS cursor never moves.

## Setup

In the game's `Cargo.toml`, make the driver an optional git dependency behind a feature:

```toml
[dependencies]
bevy_input_driver = { git = "https://github.com/halfdeadgames/bevy_input_driver", optional = true }

[features]
input_driver = ["dep:bevy_input_driver"]
```

In `main.rs`, add the plugin after `DefaultPlugins`:

```rust
#[cfg(feature = "input_driver")]
app.add_plugins(bevy_input_driver::InputDriverPlugin::default());
```

`InputDriverPlugin` has `port` (default 15702), `script_path` (default none), `enable_remote` (default true), `seed` (default none), and `update_window_cursor_position` (default false). By default the driver keeps its cursor only in `drive/status` and does not write `Window::cursor_position`, so `window.cursor_position()` stays empty; `bevy_picking` and `bevy_ui` are unaffected. Set the field to true if your game reads `window.cursor_position()`. Warning: `bevy_winit` compares each changed window with a private cache, so with the field on, any later change to the `Window` (a title or cursor icon, for example) warps your real OS cursor to the injected position. Turn it on only for unattended runs. Do not add `RemotePlugin` yourself while `enable_remote` is true: the driver adds it, with the built-in `world.*` methods still available.

Try it with the demo scene: `cargo run --example driver_demo`.

## Commands

One JSON object per command, tagged by `cmd`. Positions are logical pixels in the primary window, origin top left. Keys use Bevy `KeyCode` names, buttons use `MouseButton` names (`Left` by default).

| Command | Example |
| --- | --- |
| `key_down` | `{"cmd":"key_down","key":"KeyA","text":"a"}` |
| `key_up` | `{"cmd":"key_up","key":"KeyA"}` |
| `key_tap` | `{"cmd":"key_tap","key":"Space","hold_frames":2}` (default 1) |
| `type_text` | `{"cmd":"type_text","text":"hello"}` |
| `mouse_move` | `{"cmd":"mouse_move","x":100,"y":80}` |
| `mouse_down` / `mouse_up` | `{"cmd":"mouse_down","button":"Right"}` |
| `click` | `{"cmd":"click","x":140,"y":80}` (moves first when `x` and `y` are given) |
| `drag` | `{"cmd":"drag","from":[450,300],"to":[200,300],"frames":10}` |
| `scroll` | `{"cmd":"scroll","y":-3,"unit":"line"}` (`line` or `pixel`) |
| `touch` | `{"cmd":"touch","phase":"started","x":50,"y":60,"id":0}` (`started`, `moved`, `ended`, `canceled`) |
| `touch_drag` | `{"cmd":"touch_drag","from":[0,0],"to":[100,100],"id":0,"frames":10}` |
| `pinch` / `rotate` | `{"cmd":"pinch","delta":0.5}` |
| `pan` | `{"cmd":"pan","x":10,"y":0}` |
| `double_tap` | `{"cmd":"double_tap"}` |
| `wait_frames` | `{"cmd":"wait_frames","frames":30}` |
| `wait_seconds` | `{"cmd":"wait_seconds","seconds":0.5}` (wall-clock time from `std::time::Instant`, so pausing or `time_fixed_step` does not change it) |
| `screenshot` | `{"cmd":"screenshot","path":"shots/start.png"}` |
| `release_all` | `{"cmd":"release_all"}` |
| `quit` | `{"cmd":"quit"}` |
| `set_seed` | `{"cmd":"set_seed","seed":42}` (sets `DriverSeed`) |
| `time_pause` / `time_resume` | `{"cmd":"time_pause"}` (pauses or resumes `Time<Virtual>`) |
| `time_speed` | `{"cmd":"time_speed","factor":0.5}` (factor must be finite and above 0) |
| `time_fixed_step` | `{"cmd":"time_fixed_step","ms":16.667}` (every frame advances exactly `ms` milliseconds, above 0) |
| `time_real` | `{"cmd":"time_real"}` (automatic real-time updates and speed 1.0; does not unpause) |
| `step_frames` | `{"cmd":"step_frames","frames":5}` (resume, run exactly 5 frames, pause; at least 1) |
| `save_state` / `load_state` | `{"cmd":"save_state","path":"saves/slot1.txt"}` (runs your registered hook) |

Frame counts (`hold_frames`, `frames`, and `wait_frames`) are capped at 100000 per command; larger values are rejected. `click`, `drag`, `touch`, `touch_drag`, and `scroll` end with an extra frame so `bevy_picking`, which reads window events a frame late, has finished before the next command (such as a screenshot) runs. `clear` also cancels touches the driver started and pauses time again if a `step_frames` was running.

Every command is visible to game systems for at least one frame of its own. `key_tap` is down, wait `hold_frames`, up, wait one frame, because a press and release in one frame are invisible to `pressed()`. `drag` moves to `from`, presses, makes `frames` evenly spaced moves ending exactly at `to`, and releases.

## Live use over BRP

BRP methods: `drive/send` (one command object or an array; all are parsed before any is queued), `drive/status`, and `drive/clear` (empties the queue and releases everything the driver holds). `drive/status` reports `frame_count`, `queue_length`, `idle`, `cursor_position`, `held_keys`, `held_buttons`, `held_touches`, `pending_screenshots`, `screenshots_saved`, `errors` (last 50), and `script_loaded`, `seed`, `paused`, `speed`, `fixed_step_ms` (null under real time), `virtual_elapsed_seconds`, and `state_operations` (last 50 entries such as `saved saves/slot1.txt`). The time fields are refreshed once per frame, and `speed` reads 0 until the first frame finishes.

The helper `tools/drive.py` needs only Python 3:

```text
python tools/drive.py send '{"cmd":"click","x":140,"y":80}' --wait
python tools/drive.py send '[{"cmd":"key_tap","key":"KeyA"},{"cmd":"screenshot","path":"shots/a.png"}]' --wait
python tools/drive.py send-file tests/smoke.jsonl --wait --timeout 60
python tools/drive.py status
python tools/drive.py wait --timeout 30
python tools/drive.py clear
```

Use `--port N` before the action for a non-default port. Exit codes: 0 on success, 1 on a timeout or bad input, 2 with a plain message when the game is not reachable at the start, and 3 when the game answers with a BRP error. If the game stops answering while `--wait` or `wait` is polling after it had answered before (for example after a `quit` command), the helper prints `{"game_exited": true}` and exits 0.

## Launch file

A JSON Lines file with one command per line. Blank lines and lines starting with `//` or `#` are skipped. The whole file is parsed first: on any error nothing is queued, and the file name, line number, and problem are logged and recorded in `drive/status` under `errors`.

```text
cargo run -- --input-script tests/smoke.jsonl
```

`--input-script` overrides `InputDriverPlugin::script_path`.

## Seeds and repeatable tests

For a test to repeat exactly, the game must take all of its randomness from one random generator, built from `DriverSeed` at startup. The driver does not depend on `rand`: it only stores the number.

```rust
fn build_rng(seed: Res<bevy_input_driver::DriverSeed>, mut commands: Commands) {
    let value = seed.0.unwrap_or_else(pick_my_own_seed); // None means the game chooses
    commands.insert_resource(GameRng(StdRng::seed_from_u64(value)));
}
```

Set the seed with `--seed 42` (or `--seed=42`) on the command line, with `InputDriverPlugin { seed: Some(42), ..default() }`, or with the `set_seed` command. The argument wins over the plugin field. The resource exists before your `Startup` systems run. A game may also react to `Changed<DriverSeed>` to reseed after `set_seed`. Pair the seed with `time_fixed_step` so frame timing repeats too.

## Time control

`time_pause`, `time_resume`, and `time_speed` change `Time<Virtual>`, so only code that reads virtual time (including `FixedUpdate`) is affected. `time_fixed_step` switches `TimeUpdateStrategy` to a manual duration per frame, and `time_real` switches it back. `step_frames` expands to resume, wait the given number of frames, and pause, so call it while paused.

## Save and load hooks

Register two one-shot systems that take the command's path as `In<PathBuf>` and return `Result<(), String>`:

```rust
use bevy_input_driver::InputDriverAppExt;

app.set_driver_state_handlers(save_game_state, load_game_state);

fn save_game_state(In(path): In<PathBuf>, player: Single<&Transform, With<Player>>) -> Result<(), String> {
    std::fs::write(&path, format!("{}", player.translation.x)).map_err(|problem| problem.to_string())
}
```

`save_state` and `load_state` run the matching hook, then end the frame so the game sees the loaded state on the next frame. Success adds an entry such as `loaded saves/slot1.txt` to `state_operations`. A failure, or calling either command with no hooks registered ("no save system registered: call set_driver_state_handlers"), is recorded in `errors` with the command and path in front, and the queue continues.

## Gotchas

- `wait_seconds` uses wall-clock time, so it keeps counting while virtual time is paused. `wait_frames` counts rendered frames and also keeps running.
- `Time<Virtual>` clamps each frame's advance to its max delta (250 ms by default). A `time_fixed_step` above that raises the max delta to the step, and `time_real` restores 250 ms.

- Keep your real mouse and keyboard off the game window during tests. Real input mixes with injected input.
- Only one `RemotePlugin` may exist. The driver adds it when `enable_remote` is true.
- Screenshot paths are relative to the process working directory. Parent folders are created. Use a `.png` extension, since the format follows the extension.
- The `drive/*` methods run in `Update`, so a paused or unfocused game that stops updating also stops the driver.
- Commands with no primary window are skipped and an error is recorded.

Licensed under either of [Apache License, Version 2.0](LICENSE-APACHE) or [MIT license](LICENSE-MIT), at your option, the same as Bevy.

Unless you explicitly state otherwise, any contribution you intentionally submit for inclusion in this work, as defined in the Apache-2.0 license, shall be dual licensed as above, without any additional terms or conditions.

`examples/driver_demo.rs`:

```rs
//! Deterministic test scene for checking the input driver by eye and by screenshot.

use std::{fs, path::PathBuf};

use bevy::{
    input::{
        ButtonState,
        gestures::{DoubleTapGesture, PanGesture, PinchGesture, RotationGesture},
        keyboard::KeyboardInput,
        mouse::MouseWheel,
        touch::Touches,
    },
    prelude::*,
    window::{PrimaryWindow, WindowResolution},
};
use bevy_input_driver::{DriverSeed, InputDriverAppExt, InputDriverPlugin};

const DARK_BLUE: Color = Color::srgb(0.05, 0.1, 0.45);
const BRIGHT_GREEN: Color = Color::srgb(0.1, 0.95, 0.2);
const ORANGE: Color = Color::srgb(1.0, 0.5, 0.0);
const TEXT_SIZE: f32 = 18.0;

#[derive(Component)]
struct ToggleButton {
    is_green: bool,
}

#[derive(Component)]
struct DraggableSquare {
    top_left: Vec2,
}

#[derive(Component)]
struct TypedTextLine;

#[derive(Component)]
struct StatusTextLine;

#[derive(Resource)]
struct TypedText(String);

impl Default for TypedText {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self("typed: ".to_owned())
    }
}

#[derive(Resource, Default)]
struct InputTotals {
    scroll_y: f32,
    pinch: f32,
    rotation: f32,
    pan: Vec2,
    double_taps: u32,
}

fn main() {
    App::new()
        .add_plugins(DefaultPlugins.set(WindowPlugin {
            primary_window: Some(Window {
                title: "driver demo".to_owned(),
                resolution: WindowResolution::new(800, 600),
                resizable: false,
                ..default()
            }),
            ..default()
        }))
        .add_plugins(InputDriverPlugin::default())
        .set_driver_state_handlers(save_demo_state, load_demo_state)
        .insert_resource(ClearColor(Color::srgb(0.15, 0.15, 0.15)))
        .init_resource::<TypedText>()
        .init_resource::<InputTotals>()
        .add_systems(
            Startup,
            (
                spawn_camera,
                spawn_toggle_button,
                spawn_draggable_square,
                spawn_typed_text_line,
                spawn_status_text_line,
            ),
        )
        .add_systems(
            Update,
            (
                collect_typed_text,
                accumulate_scroll,
                accumulate_pinch,
                accumulate_rotation,
                accumulate_pan,
                count_double_taps,
                show_typed_text,
                show_status,
            ),
        )
        .run();
}

fn absolute_node(left: f32, top: f32, width: f32, height: f32) -> Node {
    Node {
        position_type: PositionType::Absolute,
        left: Val::Px(left),
        top: Val::Px(top),
        width: Val::Px(width),
        height: Val::Px(height),
        ..default()
    }
}

fn spawn_camera(mut commands: Commands) {
    commands.spawn(Camera2d);
}

fn spawn_toggle_button(mut commands: Commands) {
    commands
        .spawn((
            absolute_node(40.0, 40.0, 200.0, 80.0),
            BackgroundColor(DARK_BLUE),
            ToggleButton { is_green: false },
        ))
        .observe(toggle_button_color);
}

fn toggle_button_color(
    click: On<PointerClick>,
    mut buttons: Query<(&mut ToggleButton, &mut BackgroundColor)>,
) -> Result {
    let (mut button, mut background) = buttons.get_mut(click.event_target())?;
    button.is_green = !button.is_green;
    background.0 = if button.is_green {
        BRIGHT_GREEN
    } else {
        DARK_BLUE
    };
    Ok(())
}

fn spawn_draggable_square(mut commands: Commands) {
    let top_left = Vec2::new(400.0, 250.0);
    commands
        .spawn((
            absolute_node(top_left.x, top_left.y, 100.0, 100.0),
            BackgroundColor(ORANGE),
            DraggableSquare { top_left },
        ))
        .observe(move_square_with_drag);
}

fn move_square_with_drag(
    drag: On<PointerDrag>,
    mut squares: Query<(&mut DraggableSquare, &mut Node)>,
) -> Result {
    let (mut square, mut node) = squares.get_mut(drag.event_target())?;
    square.top_left += drag.delta;
    node.left = Val::Px(square.top_left.x);
    node.top = Val::Px(square.top_left.y);
    Ok(())
}

fn spawn_text_line(commands: &mut Commands, top: f32, marker: impl Component) {
    commands.spawn((
        Text::new(""),
        TextFont::from_font_size(TEXT_SIZE),
        Node {
            position_type: PositionType::Absolute,
            left: Val::Px(40.0),
            top: Val::Px(top),
            ..default()
        },
        marker,
    ));
}

fn spawn_typed_text_line(mut commands: Commands) {
    spawn_text_line(&mut commands, 500.0, TypedTextLine);
}

fn spawn_status_text_line(mut commands: Commands) {
    spawn_text_line(&mut commands, 540.0, StatusTextLine);
}

fn collect_typed_text(mut keyboard: MessageReader<KeyboardInput>, mut typed: ResMut<TypedText>) {
    for input in keyboard.read() {
        if input.state == ButtonState::Pressed
            && let Some(text) = &input.text
        {
            typed.0.push_str(text.as_str());
        }
    }
}

fn accumulate_scroll(mut wheel: MessageReader<MouseWheel>, mut totals: ResMut<InputTotals>) {
    totals.scroll_y += wheel.read().map(|event| event.y).sum::<f32>();
}

fn accumulate_pinch(mut pinch: MessageReader<PinchGesture>, mut totals: ResMut<InputTotals>) {
    totals.pinch += pinch.read().map(|event| event.0).sum::<f32>();
}

fn accumulate_rotation(
    mut rotation: MessageReader<RotationGesture>,
    mut totals: ResMut<InputTotals>,
) {
    totals.rotation += rotation.read().map(|event| event.0).sum::<f32>();
}

fn accumulate_pan(mut pan: MessageReader<PanGesture>, mut totals: ResMut<InputTotals>) {
    totals.pan += pan.read().map(|event| event.0).sum::<Vec2>();
}

fn count_double_taps(mut taps: MessageReader<DoubleTapGesture>, mut totals: ResMut<InputTotals>) {
    totals.double_taps += u32::try_from(taps.read().count()).unwrap_or(u32::MAX);
}

fn show_typed_text(typed: Res<TypedText>, mut line: Single<&mut Text, With<TypedTextLine>>) {
    line.0.clone_from(&typed.0);
}

fn show_status(
    keys: Res<ButtonInput<KeyCode>>,
    totals: Res<InputTotals>,
    touches: Res<Touches>,
    window: Single<&Window, With<PrimaryWindow>>,
    seed: Res<DriverSeed>,
    virtual_time: Res<Time<Virtual>>,
    mut line: Single<&mut Text, With<StatusTextLine>>,
) {
    let held = keys
        .get_pressed()
        .map(|key| format!("{key:?}"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(",");
    line.0 = format!(
        "held: [{held}] scroll_y: {} pinch: {} rotation: {} pan: {} double_taps: {} touches: {} cursor: {:?} seed: {:?} paused: {} virtual_elapsed: {:.2}",
        totals.scroll_y,
        totals.pinch,
        totals.rotation,
        totals.pan,
        totals.double_taps,
        touches.iter().count(),
        window.cursor_position(),
        seed.0,
        virtual_time.is_paused(),
        virtual_time.elapsed_secs(),
    );
}

fn save_demo_state(
    In(path): In<PathBuf>,
    square: Single<&DraggableSquare>,
    button: Single<&ToggleButton>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let text = format!(
        "{} {} {}\n",
        square.top_left.x, square.top_left.y, button.is_green
    );
    fs::write(&path, text).map_err(|problem| format!("cannot write {}: {problem}", path.display()))
}

fn parse_demo_state(text: &str) -> Result<(Vec2, bool), String> {
    let fields: Vec<&str> = text.split_whitespace().collect();
    let [x, y, is_green] = fields.as_slice() else {
        return Err(format!("expected 3 fields, found {}", fields.len()));
    };
    let parse_coordinate = |field: &str| {
        field
            .parse::<f32>()
            .map_err(|problem| format!("bad coordinate {field:?}: {problem}"))
    };
    let is_green = is_green
        .parse::<bool>()
        .map_err(|problem| format!("bad toggle {is_green:?}: {problem}"))?;
    Ok((
        Vec2::new(parse_coordinate(x)?, parse_coordinate(y)?),
        is_green,
    ))
}

fn load_demo_state(
    In(path): In<PathBuf>,
    mut square: Single<(&mut DraggableSquare, &mut Node)>,
    mut button: Single<(&mut ToggleButton, &mut BackgroundColor)>,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(&path)
        .map_err(|problem| format!("cannot read {}: {problem}", path.display()))?;
    let (top_left, is_green) = parse_demo_state(&text)?;
    let (square_state, square_node) = &mut *square;
    square_state.top_left = top_left;
    square_node.left = Val::Px(top_left.x);
    square_node.top = Val::Px(top_left.y);
    let (button_state, button_background) = &mut *button;
    button_state.is_green = is_green;
    button_background.0 = if is_green { BRIGHT_GREEN } else { DARK_BLUE };
    Ok(())
}

```

`scripts/demo_smoke.jsonl`:

```jsonl
// Smoke test for examples/driver_demo.rs. Run from the crate folder:
//   cargo run --example driver_demo -- --input-script scripts/demo_smoke.jsonl --seed 42
// Expected: 03-loaded.png matches the state saved after the first drag and click
// (green button, square at left 600, top 330), and the game quits by itself.
{"cmd":"wait_frames","frames":5}
{"cmd":"screenshot","path":"target/driver-smoke/01-start.png"}
{"cmd":"drag","from":[450,300],"to":[650,380],"frames":12}
{"cmd":"click","x":140,"y":80}
{"cmd":"save_state","path":"target/driver-smoke/state.txt"}
{"cmd":"drag","from":[650,380],"to":[250,380],"frames":12}
{"cmd":"click","x":140,"y":80}
{"cmd":"set_seed","seed":7}
{"cmd":"screenshot","path":"target/driver-smoke/02-moved.png"}
{"cmd":"load_state","path":"target/driver-smoke/state.txt"}
{"cmd":"wait_frames","frames":2}
{"cmd":"screenshot","path":"target/driver-smoke/03-loaded.png"}
{"cmd":"wait_frames","frames":5}
{"cmd":"quit"}

`src/commands.rs`:

```rs
//! Command types and parsing.

use std::fmt;

use bevy::input::{keyboard::KeyCode, mouse::MouseButton};
use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::Value;

/// A position in logical pixels in the primary window, origin top left.
pub type LogicalPoint = [f32; 2];

const DEFAULT_TAP_HOLD_FRAMES: u32 = 1;
const DEFAULT_DRAG_FRAMES: u32 = 10;
/// Largest frame count one command may use.
pub const MAX_COMMAND_FRAMES: u32 = 100_000;

fn default_hold_frames() -> u32 {
    DEFAULT_TAP_HOLD_FRAMES
}

fn default_drag_frames() -> u32 {
    DEFAULT_DRAG_FRAMES
}

fn default_mouse_button() -> MouseButton {
    MouseButton::Left
}

/// Unit of a `scroll` command.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum ScrollUnit {
    /// Wheel notches.
    #[default]
    Line,
    /// Pixels, as a trackpad reports.
    Pixel,
}

/// Phase of a `touch` command.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum TouchCommandPhase {
    /// A finger touched the screen.
    Started,
    /// A finger moved.
    Moved,
    /// A finger lifted.
    Ended,
    /// The touch was interrupted.
    Canceled,
}

/// One input or control command, as written in JSON.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "cmd", rename_all = "snake_case", deny_unknown_fields)]
pub enum DriverCommand {
    /// Press a key and keep it held.
    KeyDown {
        /// Physical key, by `KeyCode` name such as `KeyA` or `Space`.
        key: KeyCode,
        /// Text the key produces, if any.
        #[serde(default)]
        text: Option<String>,
    },
    /// Release a key.
    KeyUp {
        /// Physical key.
        key: KeyCode,
    },
    /// Press a key, hold it for some frames, then release it.
    KeyTap {
        /// Physical key.
        key: KeyCode,
        /// Text the key produces, if any.
        #[serde(default)]
        text: Option<String>,
        /// Frames the key stays down. Must be at least 1.
        #[serde(default = "default_hold_frames")]
        hold_frames: u32,
    },
    /// Type each character as a key press, one key down and one key up per frame.
    TypeText {
        /// Characters to type.
        text: String,
    },
    /// Move the cursor.
    MouseMove {
        /// Logical x.
        x: f32,
        /// Logical y.
        y: f32,
    },
    /// Press a mouse button.
    MouseDown {
        /// Button, `Left` by default.
        #[serde(default = "default_mouse_button")]
        button: MouseButton,
    },
    /// Release a mouse button.
    MouseUp {
        /// Button, `Left` by default.
        #[serde(default = "default_mouse_button")]
        button: MouseButton,
    },
    /// Press and release a mouse button, moving first when `x` and `y` are given.
    Click {
        /// Button, `Left` by default.
        #[serde(default = "default_mouse_button")]
        button: MouseButton,
        /// Logical x to move to first. Requires `y`.
        #[serde(default)]
        x: Option<f32>,
        /// Logical y to move to first. Requires `x`.
        #[serde(default)]
        y: Option<f32>,
    },
    /// Press at `from`, move in evenly spaced steps to `to`, and release.
    Drag {
        /// Start position.
        from: LogicalPoint,
        /// End position.
        to: LogicalPoint,
        /// Button, `Left` by default.
        #[serde(default = "default_mouse_button")]
        button: MouseButton,
        /// Number of moves from `from` to `to`. Must be at least 1.
        #[serde(default = "default_drag_frames")]
        frames: u32,
    },
    /// Scroll the wheel.
    Scroll {
        /// Horizontal amount.
        #[serde(default)]
        x: f32,
        /// Vertical amount.
        #[serde(default)]
        y: f32,
        /// Unit of the amounts.
        #[serde(default)]
        unit: ScrollUnit,
    },
    /// Send one touch event.
    Touch {
        /// Touch phase.
        phase: TouchCommandPhase,
        /// Logical x.
        x: f32,
        /// Logical y.
        y: f32,
        /// Finger identifier.
        #[serde(default)]
        id: u64,
    },
    /// Touch down at `from`, move in evenly spaced steps to `to`, and lift.
    TouchDrag {
        /// Start position.
        from: LogicalPoint,
        /// End position.
        to: LogicalPoint,
        /// Finger identifier.
        #[serde(default)]
        id: u64,
        /// Number of moves from `from` to `to`. Must be at least 1.
        #[serde(default = "default_drag_frames")]
        frames: u32,
    },
    /// Two-finger pinch gesture.
    Pinch {
        /// Pinch amount.
        delta: f32,
    },
    /// Two-finger rotation gesture.
    Rotate {
        /// Rotation amount.
        delta: f32,
    },
    /// Pan gesture.
    Pan {
        /// Horizontal amount.
        x: f32,
        /// Vertical amount.
        y: f32,
    },
    /// Double tap gesture.
    DoubleTap {},
    /// Wait for a number of frames.
    WaitFrames {
        /// Frames to wait.
        frames: u32,
    },
    /// Wait for wall-clock seconds, measured with `std::time::Instant` from the moment the wait
    /// starts. Pausing virtual time or `time_fixed_step` does not change its length.
    WaitSeconds {
        /// Seconds to wait.
        seconds: f32,
    },
    /// Capture the primary window to a PNG file, relative to the working directory.
    Screenshot {
        /// Output path.
        path: String,
    },
    /// Release every key and button the driver holds.
    ReleaseAll {},
    /// Write `AppExit::Success`.
    Quit {},
    /// Set `DriverSeed` to this value.
    SetSeed {
        /// The new seed.
        seed: u64,
    },
    /// Pause `Time<Virtual>`.
    TimePause {},
    /// Unpause `Time<Virtual>`.
    TimeResume {},
    /// Set the relative speed of `Time<Virtual>`.
    TimeSpeed {
        /// Speed multiplier. Must be finite and above 0.
        factor: f32,
    },
    /// Make every frame advance time by exactly this many milliseconds.
    TimeFixedStep {
        /// Milliseconds per frame. Must be finite and above 0.
        ms: f64,
    },
    /// Return to automatic real-time updates and speed 1.0, without unpausing.
    TimeReal {},
    /// From a paused state, unpause, run exactly this many frames, and pause again.
    StepFrames {
        /// Frames to run. Must be at least 1.
        frames: u32,
    },
    /// Run the registered save hook with this path.
    SaveState {
        /// File path handed to the hook.
        path: String,
    },
    /// Run the registered load hook with this path.
    LoadState {
        /// File path handed to the hook.
        path: String,
    },
}

/// Why a command could not be parsed.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum CommandParseError {
    /// The JSON was malformed or did not match any command.
    Json(serde_json::Error),
    /// The JSON matched a command but its values are not usable.
    Invalid(String),
}

impl fmt::Display for CommandParseError {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::Json(source) => write!(formatter, "{source}"),
            Self::Invalid(problem) => write!(formatter, "{problem}"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for CommandParseError {
    fn source(&self) -> Option<&(dyn std::error::Error + 'static)> {
        match self {
            Self::Json(source) => Some(source),
            Self::Invalid(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

impl DriverCommand {
    fn validate(self) -> Result<Self, CommandParseError> {
        if let Some(problem) = self.frame_cap_problem() {
            return Err(CommandParseError::Invalid(problem));
        }
        let problem = match &self {
            Self::KeyTap { hold_frames: 0, .. } => Some("key_tap hold_frames must be at least 1"),
            Self::Drag { frames: 0, .. } | Self::TouchDrag { frames: 0, .. } => {
                Some("drag frames must be at least 1")
            }
            Self::Click { x, y, .. } if x.is_some() != y.is_some() => {
                Some("click needs both x and y, or neither")
            }
            Self::WaitSeconds { seconds } if !seconds.is_finite() || *seconds < 0.0 => {
                Some("wait_seconds seconds must be a finite number of at least 0")
            }
            Self::TimeSpeed { factor } if !factor.is_finite() || *factor <= 0.0 => {
                Some("time_speed factor must be a finite number above 0")
            }
            Self::TimeFixedStep { ms } if !ms.is_finite() || *ms <= 0.0 => {
                Some("time_fixed_step ms must be a finite number above 0")
            }
            Self::StepFrames { frames: 0 } => Some("step_frames frames must be at least 1"),
            _ => None,
        };
        match problem {
            Some(message) => Err(CommandParseError::Invalid(message.to_owned())),
            None => Ok(self),
        }
    }

    // A frame count expands into that many queued steps, so an unbounded one could exhaust memory.
    fn frame_cap_problem(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let (name, frames) = match self {
            Self::KeyTap { hold_frames, .. } => ("key_tap hold_frames", *hold_frames),
            Self::Drag { frames, .. } => ("drag frames", *frames),
            Self::TouchDrag { frames, .. } => ("touch_drag frames", *frames),
            Self::StepFrames { frames } => ("step_frames frames", *frames),
            Self::WaitFrames { frames } => ("wait_frames frames", *frames),
            _ => return None,
        };
        (frames > MAX_COMMAND_FRAMES)
            .then(|| format!("{name} must be at most {MAX_COMMAND_FRAMES}, got {frames}"))
    }
}

/// Parses one command from JSON text.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`CommandParseError`] for malformed JSON, an unknown `cmd`, an unknown field, a
/// missing field, or an unusable value.
pub fn parse_command_text(text: &str) -> Result<DriverCommand, CommandParseError> {
    serde_json::from_str::<DriverCommand>(text)
        .map_err(CommandParseError::Json)?
        .validate()
}

/// Parses one command from an already decoded JSON value.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`CommandParseError`] under the same conditions as [`parse_command_text`].
pub fn parse_command_value(value: Value) -> Result<DriverCommand, CommandParseError> {
    serde_json::from_value::<DriverCommand>(value)
        .map_err(CommandParseError::Json)?
        .validate()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn parse(text: &str) -> DriverCommand {
        parse_command_text(text).expect("command should parse")
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_keyboard_commands_with_defaults() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"key_down","key":"KeyA"}"#),
            DriverCommand::KeyDown {
                key: KeyCode::KeyA,
                text: None
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"key_up","key":"Space"}"#),
            DriverCommand::KeyUp {
                key: KeyCode::Space
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"key_tap","key":"Enter"}"#),
            DriverCommand::KeyTap {
                key: KeyCode::Enter,
                text: None,
                hold_frames: 1
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"key_tap","key":"KeyB","text":"b","hold_frames":3}"#),
            DriverCommand::KeyTap {
                key: KeyCode::KeyB,
                text: Some("b".to_owned()),
                hold_frames: 3
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"type_text","text":"hi"}"#),
            DriverCommand::TypeText {
                text: "hi".to_owned()
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_mouse_commands_with_defaults() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"mouse_move","x":1.5,"y":2}"#),
            DriverCommand::MouseMove { x: 1.5, y: 2.0 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"mouse_down"}"#),
            DriverCommand::MouseDown {
                button: MouseButton::Left
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"mouse_up","button":"Right"}"#),
            DriverCommand::MouseUp {
                button: MouseButton::Right
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"click","x":10,"y":20,"button":"Middle"}"#),
            DriverCommand::Click {
                button: MouseButton::Middle,
                x: Some(10.0),
                y: Some(20.0)
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"drag","from":[1,2],"to":[3,4]}"#),
            DriverCommand::Drag {
                from: [1.0, 2.0],
                to: [3.0, 4.0],
                button: MouseButton::Left,
                frames: 10
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_scroll_touch_and_gesture_commands() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"scroll"}"#),
            DriverCommand::Scroll {
                x: 0.0,
                y: 0.0,
                unit: ScrollUnit::Line
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"scroll","y":-3,"unit":"pixel"}"#),
            DriverCommand::Scroll {
                x: 0.0,
                y: -3.0,
                unit: ScrollUnit::Pixel
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"touch","phase":"started","x":5,"y":6}"#),
            DriverCommand::Touch {
                phase: TouchCommandPhase::Started,
                x: 5.0,
                y: 6.0,
                id: 0
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"touch_drag","from":[0,0],"to":[9,9],"id":2}"#),
            DriverCommand::TouchDrag {
                from: [0.0, 0.0],
                to: [9.0, 9.0],
                id: 2,
                frames: 10
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"pinch","delta":0.5}"#),
            DriverCommand::Pinch { delta: 0.5 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"rotate","delta":-1}"#),
            DriverCommand::Rotate { delta: -1.0 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"pan","x":1,"y":2}"#),
            DriverCommand::Pan { x: 1.0, y: 2.0 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"double_tap"}"#),
            DriverCommand::DoubleTap {}
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_control_commands() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"wait_frames","frames":4}"#),
            DriverCommand::WaitFrames { frames: 4 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"wait_seconds","seconds":0.25}"#),
            DriverCommand::WaitSeconds { seconds: 0.25 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"screenshot","path":"shots/a.png"}"#),
            DriverCommand::Screenshot {
                path: "shots/a.png".to_owned()
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"release_all"}"#),
            DriverCommand::ReleaseAll {}
        );
        assert_eq!(parse(r#"{"cmd":"quit"}"#), DriverCommand::Quit {});
    }

    #[test]
    fn parses_seed_time_and_state_commands() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"set_seed","seed":18446744073709551615}"#),
            DriverCommand::SetSeed { seed: u64::MAX }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"time_pause"}"#),
            DriverCommand::TimePause {}
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"time_resume"}"#),
            DriverCommand::TimeResume {}
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"time_speed","factor":0.5}"#),
            DriverCommand::TimeSpeed { factor: 0.5 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"time_fixed_step","ms":16.5}"#),
            DriverCommand::TimeFixedStep { ms: 16.5 }
        );
        assert_eq!(parse(r#"{"cmd":"time_real"}"#), DriverCommand::TimeReal {});
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"step_frames","frames":3}"#),
            DriverCommand::StepFrames { frames: 3 }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"save_state","path":"slot.txt"}"#),
            DriverCommand::SaveState {
                path: "slot.txt".to_owned()
            }
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse(r#"{"cmd":"load_state","path":"slot.txt"}"#),
            DriverCommand::LoadState {
                path: "slot.txt".to_owned()
            }
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unusable_seed_time_and_state_values() {
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"set_seed"}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"set_seed","seed":-1}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"time_speed","factor":0}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"time_speed","factor":-2}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"time_speed"}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"time_fixed_step","ms":0}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"time_fixed_step","ms":-16}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"step_frames","frames":0}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"save_state"}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"load_state","path":"a","extra":1}"#).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unknown_command_and_unknown_field() {
        let unknown_command = parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"teleport"}"#)
            .expect_err("unknown command should fail")
            .to_string();
        assert!(unknown_command.contains("teleport"), "{unknown_command}");

        let unknown_field = parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"key_up","key":"KeyA","extra":1}"#)
            .expect_err("unknown field should fail")
            .to_string();
        assert!(unknown_field.contains("extra"), "{unknown_field}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unusable_values() {
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"key_tap","key":"KeyA","hold_frames":0}"#).is_err());
        assert!(
            parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"drag","from":[0,0],"to":[1,1],"frames":0}"#).is_err()
        );
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"click","x":1}"#).is_err());
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"wait_seconds","seconds":-1}"#).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_frame_counts_above_the_cap() {
        for text in [
            r#"{"cmd":"key_tap","key":"KeyA","hold_frames":100001}"#,
            r#"{"cmd":"drag","from":[0,0],"to":[1,1],"frames":4000000000}"#,
            r#"{"cmd":"touch_drag","from":[0,0],"to":[1,1],"frames":100001}"#,
            r#"{"cmd":"step_frames","frames":100001}"#,
            r#"{"cmd":"wait_frames","frames":100001}"#,
        ] {
            let message = parse_command_text(text)
                .expect_err("count above the cap should fail")
                .to_string();
            assert!(message.contains("at most 100000"), "{message}");
        }
        assert!(parse_command_text(r#"{"cmd":"wait_frames","frames":100000}"#).is_ok());
    }
}

`src/executor.rs`:

```rs
//! The system that runs queued steps, one frame at a time.

use std::time::Instant;

use bevy::{
    app::AppExit,
    input::{ButtonState, InputSystems, touch::TouchPhase},
    prelude::*,
};

use crate::{
    injection::{InjectionError, InputInjector},
    plugin::DriverSettings,
    screenshot::request_screenshot,
    seed::queue_set_seed,
    state_handlers::queue_state_operation,
    status::DriverStatus,
    steps::{DriverQueue, DriverStep},
    time_control::{TimeControlAction, queue_time_action},
};

/// Registers the queue, the status, and the executor system.
pub struct DriverExecutorPlugin;

impl Plugin for DriverExecutorPlugin {
    fn build(&self, app: &mut App) {
        app.init_resource::<DriverQueue>()
            .init_resource::<DriverStatus>()
            .add_systems(PreUpdate, execute_driver_steps.before(InputSystems));
    }
}

/// Runs steps until a barrier or wait, so injected input is read by `InputSystems` this frame.
pub fn execute_driver_steps(
    mut queue: ResMut<DriverQueue>,
    mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>,
    settings: Res<DriverSettings>,
    mut injector: InputInjector,
    mut commands: Commands,
    mut app_exit: MessageWriter<AppExit>,
) {
    status.frame_count += 1;
    // Wall-clock time, because `time_fixed_step` makes `Time<Real>` advance by a fixed amount.
    let now = Instant::now();
    if !queue.is_still_waiting(now) {
        while let Some(step) = queue.pop_next_step() {
            match step {
                DriverStep::EndFrame => break,
                DriverStep::BeginSteppedFrames => {
                    queue_time_action(&mut commands, TimeControlAction::Resume);
                    queue.set_stepping_frames(true);
                }
                DriverStep::EndSteppedFrames => {
                    queue_time_action(&mut commands, TimeControlAction::Pause);
                    queue.set_stepping_frames(false);
                }
                DriverStep::WaitFrames(frames) => {
                    if queue.begin_frame_wait(frames) {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                DriverStep::WaitSeconds(seconds) => {
                    if queue.begin_timed_wait(now, seconds) {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                input_step => {
                    if let Err(problem) = apply_step(
                        &input_step,
                        &mut injector,
                        &mut status,
                        &mut commands,
                        &mut app_exit,
                        settings.update_window_cursor_position,
                    ) {
                        status.record_error(problem.to_string());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    status.synchronize_with_queue(&queue);
}

fn apply_step(
    step: &DriverStep,
    injector: &mut InputInjector,
    status: &mut DriverStatus,
    commands: &mut Commands,
    app_exit: &mut MessageWriter<AppExit>,
    update_window_cursor_position: bool,
) -> Result<(), InjectionError> {
    match step {
        DriverStep::KeyDown { key, text } => {
            injector.key(*key, text.as_deref(), ButtonState::Pressed)?;
            status.note_key_down(*key);
        }
        DriverStep::KeyUp { key } => {
            injector.key(*key, None, ButtonState::Released)?;
            status.note_key_up(*key);
        }
        DriverStep::MouseMove { position } => {
            injector.move_cursor(*position, status.cursor(), update_window_cursor_position)?;
            status.set_cursor(*position);
        }
        DriverStep::MouseDown { button } => {
            injector.mouse_button(*button, ButtonState::Pressed)?;
            status.note_button_down(*button);
        }
        DriverStep::MouseUp { button } => {
            injector.mouse_button(*button, ButtonState::Released)?;
            status.note_button_up(*button);
        }
        DriverStep::Scroll { unit, x, y } => injector.scroll(*unit, *x, *y)?,
        DriverStep::Touch {
            phase,
            position,
            id,
        } => {
            injector.touch(*phase, *position, *id)?;
            status.note_touch(*phase, *id, *position);
        }
        DriverStep::Pinch { delta } => injector.pinch(*delta),
        DriverStep::Rotate { delta } => injector.rotate(*delta),
        DriverStep::Pan { delta } => injector.pan(*delta),
        DriverStep::DoubleTap => injector.double_tap(),
        DriverStep::Screenshot { path } => request_screenshot(commands, status, path.clone()),
        DriverStep::ReleaseAll => release_everything_held(injector, status)?,
        DriverStep::Quit => {
            app_exit.write(AppExit::Success);
        }
        DriverStep::SetSeed(seed) => queue_set_seed(commands, *seed),
        DriverStep::Time(action) => queue_time_action(commands, *action),
        DriverStep::State { operation, path } => {
            queue_state_operation(commands, *operation, path.clone());
        }
        DriverStep::EndFrame
        | DriverStep::WaitFrames(_)
        | DriverStep::WaitSeconds(_)
        | DriverStep::BeginSteppedFrames
        | DriverStep::EndSteppedFrames => {}
    }
    Ok(())
}

// Each item leaves its list only after its release was written, so a failure keeps the rest.
fn release_everything_held(
    injector: &mut InputInjector,
    status: &mut DriverStatus,
) -> Result<(), InjectionError> {
    while let Some(key) = status.held_keys.first().copied() {
        injector.key(key, None, ButtonState::Released)?;
        status.note_key_up(key);
    }
    while let Some(button) = status.held_buttons.first().copied() {
        injector.mouse_button(button, ButtonState::Released)?;
        status.note_button_up(button);
    }
    while let Some(touch) = status.held_touches.first().copied() {
        let position = Vec2::from(touch.position);
        injector.touch(TouchPhase::Canceled, position, touch.id)?;
        status.note_touch(TouchPhase::Canceled, touch.id, position);
    }
    Ok(())
}

```

`src/injection.rs`:

```rs
//! Writing injected input as the messages the windowing backend would write.
//!
//! Every input is written twice: as its specific message, which `bevy_input` reads, and as the
//! matching `WindowEvent`, which `bevy_picking` reads. This mirrors `bevy_winit`.

use std::fmt;

use bevy::{
    ecs::{change_detection::DetectChangesMut, system::SystemParam},
    input::{
        ButtonState,
        gestures::{DoubleTapGesture, PanGesture, PinchGesture, RotationGesture},
        keyboard::{Key, KeyCode, KeyboardInput, NativeKey},
        mouse::{MouseButton, MouseButtonInput, MouseMotion, MouseScrollUnit, MouseWheel},
        touch::{TouchInput, TouchPhase},
    },
    math::Vec2,
    prelude::*,
    window::{CursorMoved, PrimaryWindow, WindowEvent},
};

use crate::steps::character_for_key_code;

/// Why an input could not be injected.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum InjectionError {
    /// The app has no `PrimaryWindow` entity to target.
    NoPrimaryWindow,
}

impl fmt::Display for InjectionError {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::NoPrimaryWindow => {
                write!(
                    formatter,
                    "no primary window exists, so input was not injected"
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for InjectionError {}

/// Derives the logical key a physical key produces.
///
/// Named keys map to their named variant. Letters and digits become `Key::Character` of `text`,
/// or of the unshifted character when `text` is absent. Any other key with `text` becomes
/// `Key::Character`, and the rest are unidentified.
#[must_use]
pub fn logical_key_for(key_code: KeyCode, text: Option<&str>) -> Key {
    if let Some(named) = named_logical_key(key_code) {
        return named;
    }
    if let Some(text) = text {
        return Key::Character(text.into());
    }
    match character_for_key_code(key_code) {
        Some(character) => Key::Character(character.to_string().as_str().into()),
        None => Key::Unidentified(NativeKey::Unidentified),
    }
}

fn named_logical_key(key_code: KeyCode) -> Option<Key> {
    let key = match key_code {
        KeyCode::Enter => Key::Enter,
        KeyCode::Space => Key::Space,
        KeyCode::Tab => Key::Tab,
        KeyCode::Escape => Key::Escape,
        KeyCode::Backspace => Key::Backspace,
        KeyCode::Delete => Key::Delete,
        KeyCode::ArrowUp => Key::ArrowUp,
        KeyCode::ArrowDown => Key::ArrowDown,
        KeyCode::ArrowLeft => Key::ArrowLeft,
        KeyCode::ArrowRight => Key::ArrowRight,
        KeyCode::Home => Key::Home,
        KeyCode::End => Key::End,
        KeyCode::PageUp => Key::PageUp,
        KeyCode::PageDown => Key::PageDown,
        KeyCode::ShiftLeft | KeyCode::ShiftRight => Key::Shift,
        KeyCode::ControlLeft | KeyCode::ControlRight => Key::Control,
        KeyCode::AltLeft | KeyCode::AltRight => Key::Alt,
        KeyCode::SuperLeft | KeyCode::SuperRight => Key::Super,
        KeyCode::F1 => Key::F1,
        KeyCode::F2 => Key::F2,
        KeyCode::F3 => Key::F3,
        KeyCode::F4 => Key::F4,
        KeyCode::F5 => Key::F5,
        KeyCode::F6 => Key::F6,
        KeyCode::F7 => Key::F7,
        KeyCode::F8 => Key::F8,
        KeyCode::F9 => Key::F9,
        KeyCode::F10 => Key::F10,
        KeyCode::F11 => Key::F11,
        KeyCode::F12 => Key::F12,
        _ => return None,
    };
    Some(key)
}

/// System parameter that writes injected input for the primary window.
#[derive(SystemParam)]
pub struct InputInjector<'w, 's> {
    primary_window: Query<'w, 's, (Entity, &'static mut Window), With<PrimaryWindow>>,
    cursor_moved: MessageWriter<'w, CursorMoved>,
    mouse_motion: MessageWriter<'w, MouseMotion>,
    keyboard_input: MessageWriter<'w, KeyboardInput>,
    mouse_button_input: MessageWriter<'w, MouseButtonInput>,
    mouse_wheel: MessageWriter<'w, MouseWheel>,
    touch_input: MessageWriter<'w, TouchInput>,
    pinch_gesture: MessageWriter<'w, PinchGesture>,
    rotation_gesture: MessageWriter<'w, RotationGesture>,
    pan_gesture: MessageWriter<'w, PanGesture>,
    double_tap_gesture: MessageWriter<'w, DoubleTapGesture>,
    window_events: MessageWriter<'w, WindowEvent>,
}

impl InputInjector<'_, '_> {
    fn primary_window_entity(&self) -> Result<Entity, InjectionError> {
        self.primary_window
            .single()
            .map(|(entity, _)| entity)
            .map_err(|_| InjectionError::NoPrimaryWindow)
    }

    /// Moves the cursor from `previous` to `position`.
    ///
    /// The window's `cursor_position` is written only when `update_window_cursor_position` is
    /// true, and then without flagging the window as changed. Even so, `bevy_winit` compares a
    /// changed window with a private cache, so a later change to the `Window` warps the real OS
    /// cursor. That is why the write is off by default.
    ///
    /// # Errors
    ///
    /// Returns [`InjectionError::NoPrimaryWindow`] when no primary window exists.
    pub fn move_cursor(
        &mut self,
        position: Vec2,
        previous: Option<Vec2>,
        update_window_cursor_position: bool,
    ) -> Result<(), InjectionError> {
        let (window, mut window_component) = self
            .primary_window
            .single_mut()
            .map_err(|_| InjectionError::NoPrimaryWindow)?;
        if update_window_cursor_position {
            window_component
                .bypass_change_detection()
                .set_cursor_position(Some(position));
        }

        let delta = previous.map(|previous| position - previous);
        let moved = CursorMoved {
            window,
            position,
            delta,
        };
        let motion = MouseMotion {
            delta: delta.unwrap_or(Vec2::ZERO),
        };
        self.window_events
            .write(WindowEvent::CursorMoved(moved.clone()));
        self.cursor_moved.write(moved);
        self.window_events.write(WindowEvent::MouseMotion(motion));
        self.mouse_motion.write(motion);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Writes a key press or release.
    ///
    /// # Errors
    ///
    /// Returns [`InjectionError::NoPrimaryWindow`] when no primary window exists.
    pub fn key(
        &mut self,
        key_code: KeyCode,
        text: Option<&str>,
        state: ButtonState,
    ) -> Result<(), InjectionError> {
        let window = self.primary_window_entity()?;
        let input = KeyboardInput {
            key_code,
            logical_key: logical_key_for(key_code, text),
            state,
            text: text.map(Into::into),
            repeat: false,
            window,
        };
        self.window_events
            .write(WindowEvent::KeyboardInput(input.clone()));
        self.keyboard_input.write(input);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Writes a mouse button press or release.
    ///
    /// # Errors
    ///
    /// Returns [`InjectionError::NoPrimaryWindow`] when no primary window exists.
    pub fn mouse_button(
        &mut self,
        button: MouseButton,
        state: ButtonState,
    ) -> Result<(), InjectionError> {
        let window = self.primary_window_entity()?;
        let input = MouseButtonInput {
            button,
            state,
            window,
        };
        self.window_events
            .write(WindowEvent::MouseButtonInput(input));
        self.mouse_button_input.write(input);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Writes a scroll wheel movement.
    ///
    /// # Errors
    ///
    /// Returns [`InjectionError::NoPrimaryWindow`] when no primary window exists.
    pub fn scroll(&mut self, unit: MouseScrollUnit, x: f32, y: f32) -> Result<(), InjectionError> {
        let window = self.primary_window_entity()?;
        let wheel = MouseWheel {
            unit,
            x,
            y,
            window,
            phase: TouchPhase::Moved,
        };
        self.window_events.write(WindowEvent::MouseWheel(wheel));
        self.mouse_wheel.write(wheel);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Writes a touch event.
    ///
    /// # Errors
    ///
    /// Returns [`InjectionError::NoPrimaryWindow`] when no primary window exists.
    pub fn touch(
        &mut self,
        phase: TouchPhase,
        position: Vec2,
        id: u64,
    ) -> Result<(), InjectionError> {
        let window = self.primary_window_entity()?;
        let input = TouchInput {
            phase,
            position,
            window,
            force: None,
            id,
        };
        self.window_events.write(WindowEvent::TouchInput(input));
        self.touch_input.write(input);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Writes a pinch gesture.
    pub fn pinch(&mut self, delta: f32) {
        self.window_events
            .write(WindowEvent::PinchGesture(PinchGesture(delta)));
        self.pinch_gesture.write(PinchGesture(delta));
    }

    /// Writes a rotation gesture.
    pub fn rotate(&mut self, delta: f32) {
        self.window_events
            .write(WindowEvent::RotationGesture(RotationGesture(delta)));
        self.rotation_gesture.write(RotationGesture(delta));
    }

    /// Writes a pan gesture.
    pub fn pan(&mut self, delta: Vec2) {
        self.window_events
            .write(WindowEvent::PanGesture(PanGesture(delta)));
        self.pan_gesture.write(PanGesture(delta));
    }

    /// Writes a double tap gesture.
    pub fn double_tap(&mut self) {
        self.window_events
            .write(WindowEvent::DoubleTapGesture(DoubleTapGesture));
        self.double_tap_gesture.write(DoubleTapGesture);
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn letters_use_text_or_lowercase_letter() {
        assert_eq!(
            logical_key_for(KeyCode::KeyA, None),
            Key::Character("a".into())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            logical_key_for(KeyCode::KeyA, Some("A")),
            Key::Character("A".into())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn digits_are_characters() {
        assert_eq!(
            logical_key_for(KeyCode::Digit7, None),
            Key::Character("7".into())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn named_keys_map_to_named_variants() {
        assert_eq!(logical_key_for(KeyCode::Enter, Some("\n")), Key::Enter);
        assert_eq!(logical_key_for(KeyCode::Space, None), Key::Space);
        assert_eq!(logical_key_for(KeyCode::ShiftRight, None), Key::Shift);
        assert_eq!(logical_key_for(KeyCode::ArrowLeft, None), Key::ArrowLeft);
        assert_eq!(logical_key_for(KeyCode::F12, None), Key::F12);
    }

    #[test]
    fn other_keys_are_unidentified_unless_text_is_given() {
        assert_eq!(
            logical_key_for(KeyCode::Comma, None),
            Key::Unidentified(NativeKey::Unidentified)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            logical_key_for(KeyCode::Comma, Some(",")),
            Key::Character(",".into())
        );
    }
}

`src/lib.rs`:

```rs
//! Drive a running Bevy game with injected keyboard, mouse, scroll, touch, and gesture input,
//! and take screenshots.
//!
//! Commands arrive live over the Bevy Remote Protocol (`drive/send`) or from a JSON Lines file
//! named at launch with `--input-script <path>`. Each command expands into per-frame steps that
//! write the same messages the windowing backend writes, so the game cannot tell injected input
//! from real input.
//!
//! Add [`InputDriverPlugin`] to the app, typically behind a cargo feature.

#![warn(missing_docs)]

pub mod commands;
pub mod executor;
pub mod injection;
pub mod plugin;
pub mod remote;
pub mod screenshot;
pub mod script_file;
pub mod seed;
pub mod state_handlers;
pub mod status;
pub mod steps;
pub mod time_control;

pub use commands::{CommandParseError, DriverCommand};
pub use plugin::InputDriverPlugin;
pub use seed::DriverSeed;
pub use state_handlers::{DriverStateHandlers, InputDriverAppExt};
pub use status::DriverStatus;
pub use steps::{DriverQueue, DriverStep};

```

`src/plugin.rs`:

```rs
//! The public plugin.

use std::path::PathBuf;

use bevy::{prelude::*, remote::http::DEFAULT_PORT};

use crate::{
    executor::DriverExecutorPlugin,
    remote::DriverRemotePlugin,
    script_file::{load_script_file, log_driver_startup},
    seed::DriverSeedPlugin,
    state_handlers::DriverStateHandlersPlugin,
    time_control::DriverTimeControlPlugin,
};

/// Settings the startup systems read.
#[derive(Resource, Debug, Clone)]
pub struct DriverSettings {
    /// BRP HTTP port.
    pub port: u16,
    /// Whether the BRP methods are served.
    pub enable_remote: bool,
    /// Script to load at startup when `--input-script` is absent.
    pub script_path: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// Whether injected cursor moves also write `Window::cursor_position`.
    pub update_window_cursor_position: bool,
}

/// Lets an automated tester inject input and take screenshots in a running game.
///
/// When [`InputDriverPlugin::enable_remote`] is true this plugin adds `RemotePlugin` and
/// `RemoteHttpPlugin` itself, so the game must not add `RemotePlugin`. Add this plugin after
/// `DefaultPlugins`.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct InputDriverPlugin {
    /// BRP HTTP port on 127.0.0.1. Defaults to bevy_remote's default, 15702.
    pub port: u16,
    /// Script of JSON Lines commands to run at startup. The `--input-script <path>` process
    /// argument overrides it.
    pub script_path: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// Serve the `drive/*` BRP methods. Defaults to true.
    pub enable_remote: bool,
    /// Seed stored in `DriverSeed`. The `--seed <u64>` process argument overrides it.
    pub seed: Option<u64>,
    /// Also write injected cursor positions to `Window::cursor_position`, for games that call
    /// `window.cursor_position()`. Defaults to false.
    ///
    /// The write bypasses change detection, but `bevy_winit` compares each changed window with
    /// a private cache, so any later change to the `Window` (a title, a cursor icon) warps the
    /// user's real OS cursor to the injected position. Turn it on only for unattended runs.
    pub update_window_cursor_position: bool,
}

impl Default for InputDriverPlugin {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            port: DEFAULT_PORT,
            script_path: None,
            enable_remote: true,
            seed: None,
            update_window_cursor_position: false,
        }
    }
}

impl Plugin for InputDriverPlugin {
    fn build(&self, app: &mut App) {
        app.insert_resource(DriverSettings {
            port: self.port,
            enable_remote: self.enable_remote,
            script_path: self.script_path.clone(),
            update_window_cursor_position: self.update_window_cursor_position,
        })
        .add_plugins((
            DriverExecutorPlugin,
            DriverSeedPlugin { seed: self.seed },
            DriverTimeControlPlugin,
            DriverStateHandlersPlugin,
        ))
        .add_systems(Startup, (load_script_file, log_driver_startup).chain());

        if self.enable_remote {
            app.add_plugins(DriverRemotePlugin { port: self.port });
        }
    }
}

`src/remote.rs`:

```rs
//! BRP methods: `drive/send`, `drive/status`, and `drive/clear`.

use bevy::{
    prelude::*,
    remote::{BrpError, BrpResult, RemotePlugin, error_codes, http::RemoteHttpPlugin},
};
use serde_json::{Value, json};

use crate::{
    commands::{DriverCommand, parse_command_value},
    status::DriverStatus,
    steps::DriverQueue,
};

/// BRP method that queues one command or an array of commands.
pub const SEND_METHOD: &str = "drive/send";
/// BRP method that returns the driver status.
pub const STATUS_METHOD: &str = "drive/status";
/// BRP method that empties the queue and releases everything held.
pub const CLEAR_METHOD: &str = "drive/clear";

/// Adds `RemotePlugin` with the driver methods, and the HTTP transport on `port`.
///
/// The game must not add `RemotePlugin` itself while this plugin is added.
pub struct DriverRemotePlugin {
    /// TCP port on 127.0.0.1.
    pub port: u16,
}

impl Plugin for DriverRemotePlugin {
    fn build(&self, app: &mut App) {
        app.add_plugins((
            RemotePlugin::default()
                .with_method_main(SEND_METHOD, send_commands_method)
                .with_method_main(STATUS_METHOD, status_method)
                .with_method_main(CLEAR_METHOD, clear_method),
            RemoteHttpPlugin::default().with_port(self.port),
        ));
    }
}

fn invalid_params(message: String) -> BrpError {
    BrpError {
        code: error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS,
        message,
        data: None,
    }
}

/// Parses `drive/send` params: one command object, or an array of them. All or nothing.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns a [`BrpError`] with the invalid-params code naming the failing array index.
pub fn parse_send_params(params: Option<Value>) -> Result<Vec<DriverCommand>, BrpError> {
    match params {
        None | Some(Value::Null) => Err(invalid_params(
            "drive/send needs params: a command object or an array of command objects".to_owned(),
        )),
        Some(Value::Array(items)) => items
            .into_iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(index, item)| {
                parse_command_value(item)
                    .map_err(|problem| invalid_params(format!("command {index}: {problem}")))
            })
            .collect(),
        Some(single @ Value::Object(_)) => parse_command_value(single)
            .map(|command| vec![command])
            .map_err(|problem| invalid_params(format!("command: {problem}"))),
        Some(other) => Err(invalid_params(format!(
            "drive/send params must be an object or an array, got {other}"
        ))),
    }
}

fn status_json(status: &DriverStatus) -> BrpResult {
    serde_json::to_value(status).map_err(BrpError::internal)
}

fn send_commands_method(
    In(params): In<Option<Value>>,
    mut queue: ResMut<DriverQueue>,
) -> BrpResult {
    let commands = parse_send_params(params)?;
    queue.enqueue_commands(&commands);
    Ok(json!({ "queued": commands.len(), "queue_length": queue.step_count() }))
}

fn status_method(
    In(_params): In<Option<Value>>,
    mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>,
    queue: Res<DriverQueue>,
) -> BrpResult {
    status.synchronize_with_queue(&queue);
    status_json(&status)
}

fn clear_method(
    In(_params): In<Option<Value>>,
    mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>,
    mut queue: ResMut<DriverQueue>,
) -> BrpResult {
    queue.clear_and_release_all();
    status.synchronize_with_queue(&queue);
    status_json(&status)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn accepts_one_object_or_an_array() {
        let one = parse_send_params(Some(json!({"cmd": "quit"}))).expect("object should parse");
        assert_eq!(one, vec![DriverCommand::Quit {}]);
        let many = parse_send_params(Some(json!([{"cmd": "quit"}, {"cmd": "release_all"}])))
            .expect("array should parse");
        assert_eq!(many.len(), 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_everything_when_one_command_is_bad() {
        let error = parse_send_params(Some(json!([{"cmd": "quit"}, {"cmd": "nope"}])))
            .expect_err("bad command should fail");
        assert_eq!(error.code, error_codes::INVALID_PARAMS);
        assert!(error.message.contains("command 1"), "{}", error.message);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_missing_and_scalar_params() {
        assert!(parse_send_params(None).is_err());
        assert!(parse_send_params(Some(json!(5))).is_err());
    }
}

`src/screenshot.rs`:

```rs
//! Screenshot requests and saving.

use std::{fmt, fs, io, path::Path};

use bevy::{
    image::Image,
    prelude::*,
    render::view::screenshot::{Screenshot, ScreenshotCaptured},
};

use crate::status::DriverStatus;

/// Why a captured screenshot could not be written.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ScreenshotSaveError {
    /// The parent folder could not be created.
    CreateFolder(io::Error),
    /// The captured pixels are in a format that cannot be converted to an image file.
    ConvertImage(String),
    /// Encoding or writing the image file failed.
    WriteFile(String),
}

impl fmt::Display for ScreenshotSaveError {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::CreateFolder(source) => write!(formatter, "cannot create folder: {source}"),
            Self::ConvertImage(source) => write!(formatter, "cannot convert image: {source}"),
            Self::WriteFile(source) => write!(formatter, "cannot write file: {source}"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ScreenshotSaveError {}

/// Queues a primary window capture and records it as pending.
///
/// When the capture arrives, the image is saved to `path` (the format follows the file
/// extension, so use `.png`) and [`DriverStatus`] is updated with the result.
pub fn request_screenshot(commands: &mut Commands, status: &mut DriverStatus, path: String) {
    status.pending_screenshots.push(path.clone());
    commands.spawn(Screenshot::primary_window()).observe(
        move |captured: On<ScreenshotCaptured>, mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>| match save_image(
            captured.image.clone(),
            Path::new(&path),
        ) {
            Ok(()) => status.note_screenshot_saved(&path),
            Err(problem) => status.note_screenshot_failed(&path, &problem.to_string()),
        },
    );
}

fn save_image(image: Image, path: &Path) -> Result<(), ScreenshotSaveError> {
    if let Some(folder) = path
        .parent()
        .filter(|folder| !folder.as_os_str().is_empty())
    {
        fs::create_dir_all(folder).map_err(ScreenshotSaveError::CreateFolder)?;
    }
    let dynamic_image = image
        .try_into_dynamic()
        .map_err(|source| ScreenshotSaveError::ConvertImage(source.to_string()))?;
    dynamic_image
        .to_rgb8()
        .save(path)
        .map_err(|source| ScreenshotSaveError::WriteFile(source.to_string()))
}

```

`src/script_file.rs`:

```rs
//! Loading a JSON Lines command script named at launch.

use std::{
    fmt, fs, io,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
};

use bevy::{log::info, prelude::*};

use crate::{
    commands::{CommandParseError, DriverCommand, parse_command_text},
    plugin::DriverSettings,
    status::DriverStatus,
    steps::DriverQueue,
};

const SCRIPT_ARGUMENT: &str = "--input-script";

/// Why a launch script could not be used.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ScriptFileError {
    /// `--input-script` was the last argument, with no path after it.
    MissingPathArgument,
    /// The file could not be read.
    Read {
        /// Script path.
        path: PathBuf,
        /// IO failure.
        source: io::Error,
    },
    /// A line is not a valid command.
    Line {
        /// Script path.
        path: PathBuf,
        /// One-based line number.
        line: usize,
        /// Parse failure.
        source: CommandParseError,
    },
}

impl fmt::Display for ScriptFileError {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::MissingPathArgument => {
                write!(formatter, "{SCRIPT_ARGUMENT} needs a file path after it")
            }
            Self::Read { path, source } => {
                write!(
                    formatter,
                    "script {}: cannot read file: {source}",
                    path.display()
                )
            }
            Self::Line { path, line, source } => {
                write!(formatter, "script {} line {line}: {source}", path.display())
            }
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for ScriptFileError {}

/// Finds the script path in process arguments, as `--input-script <path>` or
/// `--input-script=<path>`. The first argument is the program name and is ignored by callers.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`ScriptFileError::MissingPathArgument`] when the flag has no value.
pub fn script_path_from_arguments(
    arguments: impl IntoIterator<Item = String>,
) -> Result<Option<PathBuf>, ScriptFileError> {
    let mut arguments = arguments.into_iter();
    while let Some(argument) = arguments.next() {
        if argument == SCRIPT_ARGUMENT {
            return arguments
                .next()
                .map(|path| Some(PathBuf::from(path)))
                .ok_or(ScriptFileError::MissingPathArgument);
        }
        if let Some(path) = argument.strip_prefix("--input-script=") {
            return Ok(Some(PathBuf::from(path)));
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

/// Parses script text: blank lines and lines starting with `//` or `#` are skipped, every other
/// line is one command. Parses everything before returning anything.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`ScriptFileError::Line`] naming the first bad line.
pub fn parse_script_text(path: &Path, text: &str) -> Result<Vec<DriverCommand>, ScriptFileError> {
    let mut commands = Vec::new();
    for (index, raw_line) in text.lines().enumerate() {
        let line = raw_line.trim();
        if line.is_empty() || line.starts_with("//") || line.starts_with('#') {
            continue;
        }
        let command = parse_command_text(line).map_err(|source| ScriptFileError::Line {
            path: path.to_path_buf(),
            line: index + 1,
            source,
        })?;
        commands.push(command);
    }
    Ok(commands)
}

fn load_script_commands(path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<DriverCommand>, ScriptFileError> {
    let text = fs::read_to_string(path).map_err(|source| ScriptFileError::Read {
        path: path.to_path_buf(),
        source,
    })?;
    parse_script_text(path, &text)
}

/// Startup system: loads the launch script, if one is named, and queues its commands.
///
/// The `--input-script` process argument overrides the path set on the plugin. On any error
/// nothing is queued and the error is recorded in [`DriverStatus::errors`].
pub fn load_script_file(
    settings: Res<DriverSettings>,
    mut queue: ResMut<DriverQueue>,
    mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>,
) {
    let from_arguments = match script_path_from_arguments(std::env::args().skip(1)) {
        Ok(path) => path,
        Err(problem) => {
            status.record_error(problem.to_string());
            return;
        }
    };
    let Some(path) = from_arguments.or_else(|| settings.script_path.clone()) else {
        return;
    };
    match load_script_commands(&path) {
        Ok(commands) => {
            queue.enqueue_commands(&commands);
            status.script_loaded = Some(path.display().to_string());
        }
        Err(problem) => status.record_error(problem.to_string()),
    }
}

/// Startup system: logs the listening address and whether a script was loaded.
pub fn log_driver_startup(settings: Res<DriverSettings>, status: Res<DriverStatus>) {
    let listening = if settings.enable_remote {
        format!("listening on 127.0.0.1:{}", settings.port)
    } else {
        "remote control disabled".to_owned()
    };
    let script = status.script_loaded.as_deref().map_or_else(
        || "no script loaded".to_owned(),
        |path| format!("script loaded: {path}"),
    );
    info!("input driver {listening}, {script}");
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn arguments(values: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {
        values.iter().map(|value| (*value).to_owned()).collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn skips_comments_and_blank_lines() {
        let text = "// header\n\n# note\n{\"cmd\":\"quit\"}\n   \n{\"cmd\":\"release_all\"}\n";
        let commands = parse_script_text(Path::new("t.jsonl"), text).expect("script should parse");
        assert_eq!(
            commands,
            vec![DriverCommand::Quit {}, DriverCommand::ReleaseAll {}]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn errors_name_file_and_line() {
        let text = "// c\n{\"cmd\":\"quit\"}\n{\"cmd\":\"bogus\"}\n";
        let message = parse_script_text(Path::new("t.jsonl"), text)
            .expect_err("bad line should fail")
            .to_string();
        assert!(message.contains("t.jsonl"), "{message}");
        assert!(message.contains("line 3"), "{message}");
        assert!(message.contains("bogus"), "{message}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn finds_script_argument_in_both_forms() {
        assert_eq!(
            script_path_from_arguments(arguments(&["--input-script", "a.jsonl"]))
                .expect("flag with value"),
            Some(PathBuf::from("a.jsonl"))
        );
        assert_eq!(
            script_path_from_arguments(arguments(&["--input-script=b.jsonl"]))
                .expect("flag with equals"),
            Some(PathBuf::from("b.jsonl"))
        );
        assert_eq!(
            script_path_from_arguments(arguments(&["--other"])).expect("no flag"),
            None
        );
        assert!(script_path_from_arguments(arguments(&["--input-script"])).is_err());
    }
}

`src/seed.rs`:

```rs
//! The seed a game uses to make its randomness repeatable.

use std::fmt;

use bevy::prelude::*;

use crate::status::DriverStatus;

const SEED_ARGUMENT: &str = "--seed";

/// Seed for the game's random number generator, set by `--seed <u64>`, the plugin's `seed`
/// field, or the `set_seed` command.
///
/// Contract for games: build your single seeded random generator from this resource at
/// startup, and optionally reseed it when `Changed<DriverSeed>` fires. When it holds `None`,
/// pick your own seed. The driver does not depend on any random number crate.
#[derive(Resource, Debug, Default, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct DriverSeed(pub Option<u64>);

/// Why a `--seed` argument could not be used.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SeedArgumentError {
    /// `--seed` was the last argument, with no value after it.
    MissingValue,
    /// The value is not an unsigned 64-bit integer.
    InvalidValue(String),
}

impl fmt::Display for SeedArgumentError {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::MissingValue => write!(formatter, "{SEED_ARGUMENT} needs a number after it"),
            Self::InvalidValue(value) => write!(
                formatter,
                "{SEED_ARGUMENT} value {value:?} is not an unsigned 64-bit integer"
            ),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for SeedArgumentError {}

fn parse_seed_value(value: &str) -> Result<u64, SeedArgumentError> {
    value
        .parse::<u64>()
        .map_err(|_| SeedArgumentError::InvalidValue(value.to_owned()))
}

/// Finds the seed in process arguments, as `--seed <u64>` or `--seed=<u64>`. The first
/// argument is the program name and is ignored by callers.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`SeedArgumentError`] when the flag has no value or the value is not a `u64`.
pub fn seed_from_arguments(
    arguments: impl IntoIterator<Item = String>,
) -> Result<Option<u64>, SeedArgumentError> {
    let mut arguments = arguments.into_iter();
    while let Some(argument) = arguments.next() {
        if argument == SEED_ARGUMENT {
            let value = arguments.next().ok_or(SeedArgumentError::MissingValue)?;
            return parse_seed_value(&value).map(Some);
        }
        if let Some(value) = argument.strip_prefix("--seed=") {
            return parse_seed_value(value).map(Some);
        }
    }
    Ok(None)
}

/// A `--seed` problem found while building the plugin, reported once the status exists.
#[derive(Resource, Debug)]
struct SeedArgumentProblem(Option<String>);

/// Inserts [`DriverSeed`] and reports it in the status.
pub struct DriverSeedPlugin {
    /// Seed used when no `--seed` argument is given.
    pub seed: Option<u64>,
}

impl Plugin for DriverSeedPlugin {
    fn build(&self, app: &mut App) {
        let (argument_seed, problem) = match seed_from_arguments(std::env::args().skip(1)) {
            Ok(seed) => (seed, None),
            Err(problem) => (None, Some(problem.to_string())),
        };
        app.insert_resource(DriverSeed(argument_seed.or(self.seed)))
            .insert_resource(SeedArgumentProblem(problem))
            .add_systems(Startup, report_seed_argument_problem)
            .add_systems(
                Last,
                report_seed_in_status.run_if(resource_changed::<DriverSeed>),
            );
    }
}

fn report_seed_argument_problem(
    problem: Res<SeedArgumentProblem>,
    mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>,
) {
    if let Some(message) = &problem.0 {
        status.record_error(message.clone());
    }
}

fn report_seed_in_status(seed: Res<DriverSeed>, mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>) {
    status.seed = seed.0;
}

/// Queues a world command that makes `seed` the current [`DriverSeed`].
pub fn queue_set_seed(commands: &mut Commands, seed: u64) {
    commands.queue(move |world: &mut World| {
        world.insert_resource(DriverSeed(Some(seed)));
    });
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn arguments(values: &[&str]) -> Vec<String> {
        values.iter().map(|value| (*value).to_owned()).collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn finds_seed_argument_in_both_forms() {
        assert_eq!(
            seed_from_arguments(arguments(&["--seed", "42"])),
            Ok(Some(42))
        );
        assert_eq!(seed_from_arguments(arguments(&["--seed=7"])), Ok(Some(7)));
        assert_eq!(
            seed_from_arguments(arguments(&["--other", "--seed", "18446744073709551615"])),
            Ok(Some(u64::MAX))
        );
        assert_eq!(seed_from_arguments(arguments(&["--other"])), Ok(None));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_missing_and_invalid_seed_values() {
        assert_eq!(
            seed_from_arguments(arguments(&["--seed"])),
            Err(SeedArgumentError::MissingValue)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            seed_from_arguments(arguments(&["--seed", "-3"])),
            Err(SeedArgumentError::InvalidValue("-3".to_owned()))
        );
        assert!(seed_from_arguments(arguments(&["--seed=abc"])).is_err());
    }
}
