`src/state_handlers.rs`:

```rs
//! Game-provided save and load hooks, run by the `save_state` and `load_state` commands.

use std::{fmt, path::PathBuf};

use bevy::{ecs::system::SystemId, prelude::*};

use crate::status::{DriverStatus, record_error_in_world};

/// One-shot system id of a hook that receives the file path from the command.
pub type StateHandlerId = SystemId<In<PathBuf>, Result<(), String>>;

/// The registered save and load hooks. Fill it with
/// [`InputDriverAppExt::set_driver_state_handlers`].
#[derive(Resource, Debug, Default, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct DriverStateHandlers {
    /// Hook run by `save_state`.
    pub save: Option<StateHandlerId>,
    /// Hook run by `load_state`.
    pub load: Option<StateHandlerId>,
}

/// Which hook a state command runs.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum StateOperation {
    /// Run the save hook.
    Save,
    /// Run the load hook.
    Load,
}

impl StateOperation {
    fn command_name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Save => "save_state",
            Self::Load => "load_state",
        }
    }

    fn past_tense(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Save => "saved",
            Self::Load => "loaded",
        }
    }

    fn hook_name(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Save => "save",
            Self::Load => "load",
        }
    }
}

/// Why a state operation failed.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum StateOperationError {
    /// No hook of this kind is registered.
    NoHandler(StateOperation),
    /// Bevy could not run the registered system.
    CannotRun(String),
    /// The hook ran and returned an error.
    HandlerFailed(String),
}

impl fmt::Display for StateOperationError {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::NoHandler(operation) => write!(
                formatter,
                "no {} system registered: call set_driver_state_handlers",
                operation.hook_name()
            ),
            Self::CannotRun(problem) => write!(formatter, "cannot run handler: {problem}"),
            Self::HandlerFailed(problem) => write!(formatter, "handler failed: {problem}"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for StateOperationError {}

/// Runs the registered hook for `operation` with `path`.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`StateOperationError`] when no hook is registered, the hook cannot run, or the
/// hook returns an error.
pub fn run_state_operation(
    world: &mut World,
    operation: StateOperation,
    path: &str,
) -> Result<(), StateOperationError> {
    let handlers = world
        .get_resource::<DriverStateHandlers>()
        .copied()
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let handler = match operation {
        StateOperation::Save => handlers.save,
        StateOperation::Load => handlers.load,
    }
    .ok_or(StateOperationError::NoHandler(operation))?;
    world
        .run_system_with::<In<PathBuf>, Result<(), String>>(handler, PathBuf::from(path))
        .map_err(|problem| StateOperationError::CannotRun(problem.to_string()))?
        .map_err(StateOperationError::HandlerFailed)
}

/// Queues a world command that runs the hook and records the outcome in [`DriverStatus`].
pub fn queue_state_operation(commands: &mut Commands, operation: StateOperation, path: String) {
    commands.queue(
        move |world: &mut World| match run_state_operation(world, operation, &path) {
            Ok(()) => world
                .resource_mut::<DriverStatus>()
                .note_state_operation(format!("{} {path}", operation.past_tense())),
            Err(problem) => record_error_in_world(
                world,
                format!("{} {path}: {problem}", operation.command_name()),
            ),
        },
    );
}

/// Registers [`DriverStateHandlers`].
pub struct DriverStateHandlersPlugin;

impl Plugin for DriverStateHandlersPlugin {
    fn build(&self, app: &mut App) {
        app.init_resource::<DriverStateHandlers>();
    }
}

/// App methods for connecting a game's save and load code to the driver.
pub trait InputDriverAppExt {
    /// Registers the systems that `save_state` and `load_state` run.
    ///
    /// Each system receives the file path from the command as `In<PathBuf>` and returns
    /// `Ok(())` or a message describing why it failed. Both run as one-shot systems, so they
    /// may take any ordinary system parameters after the `In` input.
    fn set_driver_state_handlers<SaveMarker, LoadMarker>(
        &mut self,
        save_system: impl IntoSystem<In<PathBuf>, Result<(), String>, SaveMarker> + 'static,
        load_system: impl IntoSystem<In<PathBuf>, Result<(), String>, LoadMarker> + 'static,
    ) -> &mut Self;
}

impl InputDriverAppExt for App {
    fn set_driver_state_handlers<SaveMarker, LoadMarker>(
        &mut self,
        save_system: impl IntoSystem<In<PathBuf>, Result<(), String>, SaveMarker> + 'static,
        load_system: impl IntoSystem<In<PathBuf>, Result<(), String>, LoadMarker> + 'static,
    ) -> &mut Self {
        let save = self.register_system(save_system);
        let load = self.register_system(load_system);
        self.insert_resource(DriverStateHandlers {
            save: Some(save),
            load: Some(load),
        })
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[derive(Resource, Default)]
    struct LastPath(Option<PathBuf>);

    fn remember_path(In(path): In<PathBuf>, mut last: ResMut<LastPath>) -> Result<(), String> {
        last.0 = Some(path);
        Ok(())
    }

    fn always_fail(In(_path): In<PathBuf>) -> Result<(), String> {
        Err("disk full".to_owned())
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_handlers_name_the_fix() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        let problem = run_state_operation(&mut world, StateOperation::Save, "a.txt")
            .expect_err("no handler is registered");
        assert_eq!(
            problem,
            StateOperationError::NoHandler(StateOperation::Save)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            problem.to_string(),
            "no save system registered: call set_driver_state_handlers"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn runs_the_matching_handler_with_the_path() {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.init_resource::<LastPath>();
        let handlers = DriverStateHandlers {
            save: Some(world.register_system(remember_path)),
            load: Some(world.register_system(always_fail)),
        };
        world.insert_resource(handlers);

        run_state_operation(&mut world, StateOperation::Save, "slot.txt").expect("save works");
        assert_eq!(
            world.resource::<LastPath>().0.as_deref(),
            Some(std::path::Path::new("slot.txt"))
        );
        assert_eq!(
            run_state_operation(&mut world, StateOperation::Load, "slot.txt"),
            Err(StateOperationError::HandlerFailed("disk full".to_owned()))
        );
    }
}
```

`src/status.rs`:

```rs
//! Observable driver state, served by `drive/status`.

use bevy::{
    input::{keyboard::KeyCode, mouse::MouseButton, touch::TouchPhase},
    log::error,
    math::Vec2,
    prelude::{Resource, World},
};
use serde::Serialize;

use crate::steps::DriverQueue;

const MAX_RECORDED_ERRORS: usize = 50;
const MAX_RECORDED_STATE_OPERATIONS: usize = 50;

/// A finger the driver put down and has not lifted.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Serialize)]
pub struct HeldTouch {
    /// Finger identifier.
    pub id: u64,
    /// Last logical position, used when the touch is canceled.
    pub position: [f32; 2],
}

/// What the driver has done and what it is still doing.
#[derive(Resource, Debug, Default, Serialize)]
pub struct DriverStatus {
    /// Frames the executor has run.
    pub frame_count: u64,
    /// Steps still waiting in the queue.
    pub queue_length: usize,
    /// True when the queue is empty, no wait is active, and no screenshot is pending.
    pub idle: bool,
    /// Driver cursor in logical pixels, once it has moved.
    pub cursor_position: Option<[f32; 2]>,
    /// Keys the driver pressed and has not released.
    pub held_keys: Vec<KeyCode>,
    /// Mouse buttons the driver pressed and has not released.
    pub held_buttons: Vec<MouseButton>,
    /// Fingers the driver started and has not ended or canceled.
    pub held_touches: Vec<HeldTouch>,
    /// Screenshot paths requested but not yet written.
    pub pending_screenshots: Vec<String>,
    /// Screenshot paths written successfully.
    pub screenshots_saved: Vec<String>,
    /// The most recent 50 errors.
    pub errors: Vec<String>,
    /// Path of the launch script that was loaded, if any.
    pub script_loaded: Option<String>,
    /// The seed in `DriverSeed`, if one is set.
    pub seed: Option<u64>,
    /// Whether `Time<Virtual>` is paused. Refreshed once per frame.
    pub paused: bool,
    /// Relative speed of `Time<Virtual>`. Refreshed once per frame, so it reads 0 until the
    /// first frame has finished.
    pub speed: f32,
    /// Milliseconds every frame advances by under `time_fixed_step`, or none under real time.
    pub fixed_step_ms: Option<f64>,
    /// Seconds elapsed on `Time<Virtual>`. Refreshed once per frame.
    pub virtual_elapsed_seconds: f64,
    /// The most recent 50 finished `save_state` and `load_state` operations.
    pub state_operations: Vec<String>,
}

/// Logs an error and records it in [`DriverStatus`] from code that only has the world.
pub fn record_error_in_world(world: &mut World, message: String) {
    match world.get_resource_mut::<DriverStatus>() {
        Some(mut status) => status.record_error(message),
        None => error!("input driver: {message}"),
    }
}

impl DriverStatus {
    /// Records a finished `save_state` or `load_state`, dropping the oldest past 50.
    pub fn note_state_operation(&mut self, description: String) {
        self.state_operations.push(description);
        let excess = self
            .state_operations
            .len()
            .saturating_sub(MAX_RECORDED_STATE_OPERATIONS);
        self.state_operations.drain(..excess);
    }
}

impl DriverStatus {
    /// Copies queue-derived fields so a reader sees the queue as it is right now.
    pub fn synchronize_with_queue(&mut self, queue: &DriverQueue) {
        self.queue_length = queue.step_count();
        self.idle = queue.is_idle() && self.pending_screenshots.is_empty();
    }

    /// Logs an error and keeps it in [`DriverStatus::errors`], dropping the oldest past 50.
    pub fn record_error(&mut self, message: impl Into<String>) {
        let message = message.into();
        error!("input driver: {message}");
        self.errors.push(message);
        let excess = self.errors.len().saturating_sub(MAX_RECORDED_ERRORS);
        self.errors.drain(..excess);
    }

    /// The driver cursor as a vector.
    #[must_use]
    pub fn cursor(&self) -> Option<Vec2> {
        self.cursor_position.map(Vec2::from)
    }

    /// Remembers the driver cursor.
    pub fn set_cursor(&mut self, position: Vec2) {
        self.cursor_position = Some(position.to_array());
    }

    /// Records that the driver pressed `key`.
    pub fn note_key_down(&mut self, key: KeyCode) {
        if !self.held_keys.contains(&key) {
            self.held_keys.push(key);
        }
    }

    /// Records that the driver released `key`.
    pub fn note_key_up(&mut self, key: KeyCode) {
        self.held_keys.retain(|held| *held != key);
    }

    /// Records that the driver pressed `button`.
    pub fn note_button_down(&mut self, button: MouseButton) {
        if !self.held_buttons.contains(&button) {
            self.held_buttons.push(button);
        }
    }

    /// Records that the driver released `button`.
    pub fn note_button_up(&mut self, button: MouseButton) {
        self.held_buttons.retain(|held| *held != button);
    }

    /// Records that the driver touched, moved, or lifted the finger `id` at `position`.
    pub fn note_touch(&mut self, phase: TouchPhase, id: u64, position: Vec2) {
        let existing = self.held_touches.iter().position(|touch| touch.id == id);
        match (phase, existing) {
            (TouchPhase::Started | TouchPhase::Moved, Some(index)) => {
                self.held_touches[index].position = position.to_array();
            }
            (TouchPhase::Started, None) => self.held_touches.push(HeldTouch {
                id,
                position: position.to_array(),
            }),
            (TouchPhase::Ended | TouchPhase::Canceled, Some(index)) => {
                self.held_touches.remove(index);
            }
            (TouchPhase::Moved | TouchPhase::Ended | TouchPhase::Canceled, None) => {}
        }
    }

    /// Records a finished screenshot and removes one matching pending entry.
    pub fn note_screenshot_saved(&mut self, path: &str) {
        self.remove_one_pending_screenshot(path);
        self.screenshots_saved.push(path.to_owned());
    }

    /// Removes one matching screenshot from the pending list after it failed.
    pub fn note_screenshot_failed(&mut self, path: &str, problem: &str) {
        self.remove_one_pending_screenshot(path);
        self.record_error(format!("screenshot {path}: {problem}"));
    }

    // Two requests for one path are two captures, so each result clears only one entry.
    fn remove_one_pending_screenshot(&mut self, path: &str) {
        if let Some(index) = self
            .pending_screenshots
            .iter()
            .position(|pending| pending == path)
        {
            self.pending_screenshots.remove(index);
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn each_screenshot_result_clears_only_one_pending_entry() {
        let mut status = DriverStatus {
            pending_screenshots: vec!["a.png".to_owned(), "a.png".to_owned()],
            ..DriverStatus::default()
        };
        status.note_screenshot_saved("a.png");
        assert_eq!(status.pending_screenshots, vec!["a.png".to_owned()]);
        status.note_screenshot_failed("a.png", "disk full");
        assert!(status.pending_screenshots.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(status.screenshots_saved, vec!["a.png".to_owned()]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn touches_are_held_from_start_until_end_or_cancel() {
        let mut status = DriverStatus::default();
        status.note_touch(TouchPhase::Started, 1, Vec2::new(1.0, 2.0));
        status.note_touch(TouchPhase::Started, 2, Vec2::ZERO);
        status.note_touch(TouchPhase::Moved, 1, Vec2::new(5.0, 6.0));
        assert_eq!(status.held_touches.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(status.held_touches[0].position, [5.0, 6.0]);
        status.note_touch(TouchPhase::Ended, 1, Vec2::ZERO);
        status.note_touch(TouchPhase::Canceled, 2, Vec2::ZERO);
        assert!(status.held_touches.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn keeps_only_the_last_fifty_errors() {
        let mut status = DriverStatus::default();
        for index in 0..60 {
            status.record_error(format!("error {index}"));
        }
        assert_eq!(status.errors.len(), 50);
        assert_eq!(status.errors.first().map(String::as_str), Some("error 10"));
    }
}

```

`src/steps.rs`:

```rs
//! Expanding commands into per-frame steps, and the queue that holds them.

use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::iter::repeat_n;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use bevy::{
    input::{
        keyboard::{KeyCode, NativeKeyCode},
        mouse::{MouseButton, MouseScrollUnit},
        touch::TouchPhase,
    },
    math::Vec2,
    prelude::Resource,
};

use crate::{
    commands::{DriverCommand, ScrollUnit, TouchCommandPhase},
    state_handlers::StateOperation,
    time_control::TimeControlAction,
};

const CHARACTER_KEYS: [(char, KeyCode); 36] = [
    ('a', KeyCode::KeyA),
    ('b', KeyCode::KeyB),
    ('c', KeyCode::KeyC),
    ('d', KeyCode::KeyD),
    ('e', KeyCode::KeyE),
    ('f', KeyCode::KeyF),
    ('g', KeyCode::KeyG),
    ('h', KeyCode::KeyH),
    ('i', KeyCode::KeyI),
    ('j', KeyCode::KeyJ),
    ('k', KeyCode::KeyK),
    ('l', KeyCode::KeyL),
    ('m', KeyCode::KeyM),
    ('n', KeyCode::KeyN),
    ('o', KeyCode::KeyO),
    ('p', KeyCode::KeyP),
    ('q', KeyCode::KeyQ),
    ('r', KeyCode::KeyR),
    ('s', KeyCode::KeyS),
    ('t', KeyCode::KeyT),
    ('u', KeyCode::KeyU),
    ('v', KeyCode::KeyV),
    ('w', KeyCode::KeyW),
    ('x', KeyCode::KeyX),
    ('y', KeyCode::KeyY),
    ('z', KeyCode::KeyZ),
    ('0', KeyCode::Digit0),
    ('1', KeyCode::Digit1),
    ('2', KeyCode::Digit2),
    ('3', KeyCode::Digit3),
    ('4', KeyCode::Digit4),
    ('5', KeyCode::Digit5),
    ('6', KeyCode::Digit6),
    ('7', KeyCode::Digit7),
    ('8', KeyCode::Digit8),
    ('9', KeyCode::Digit9),
];

/// Maps a typed character to the physical key that produces it, or an unidentified key.
#[must_use]
pub fn key_code_for_character(character: char) -> KeyCode {
    match character {
        ' ' => KeyCode::Space,
        '\n' => KeyCode::Enter,
        other => {
            let lowercase = other.to_ascii_lowercase();
            CHARACTER_KEYS
                .iter()
                .find(|(candidate, _)| *candidate == lowercase)
                .map_or(
                    KeyCode::Unidentified(NativeKeyCode::Unidentified),
                    |(_, key)| *key,
                )
        }
    }
}

/// The unshifted character a letter or digit key produces.
#[must_use]
pub fn character_for_key_code(key_code: KeyCode) -> Option<char> {
    CHARACTER_KEYS
        .iter()
        .find(|(_, candidate)| *candidate == key_code)
        .map(|(character, _)| *character)
}

/// One primitive action the executor performs.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum DriverStep {
    /// Press a key.
    KeyDown {
        /// Physical key.
        key: KeyCode,
        /// Text the key produces.
        text: Option<String>,
    },
    /// Release a key.
    KeyUp {
        /// Physical key.
        key: KeyCode,
    },
    /// Move the cursor.
    MouseMove {
        /// Logical position.
        position: Vec2,
    },
    /// Press a mouse button.
    MouseDown {
        /// Button.
        button: MouseButton,
    },
    /// Release a mouse button.
    MouseUp {
        /// Button.
        button: MouseButton,
    },
    /// Scroll the wheel.
    Scroll {
        /// Unit of the amounts.
        unit: MouseScrollUnit,
        /// Horizontal amount.
        x: f32,
        /// Vertical amount.
        y: f32,
    },
    /// Send a touch event.
    Touch {
        /// Phase.
        phase: TouchPhase,
        /// Logical position.
        position: Vec2,
        /// Finger identifier.
        id: u64,
    },
    /// Pinch gesture.
    Pinch {
        /// Amount.
        delta: f32,
    },
    /// Rotation gesture.
    Rotate {
        /// Amount.
        delta: f32,
    },
    /// Pan gesture.
    Pan {
        /// Amount.
        delta: Vec2,
    },
    /// Double tap gesture.
    DoubleTap,
    /// Capture the primary window.
    Screenshot {
        /// Output path.
        path: String,
    },
    /// Release everything the driver holds.
    ReleaseAll,
    /// Exit the app.
    Quit,
    /// Make this the current `DriverSeed`.
    SetSeed(u64),
    /// Change the game clock.
    Time(TimeControlAction),
    /// Run the save or load hook.
    State {
        /// Which hook to run.
        operation: StateOperation,
        /// File path handed to the hook.
        path: String,
    },
    /// Stop executing until the next frame so game systems see the steps so far.
    EndFrame,
    /// Skip this many frames.
    WaitFrames(u32),
    /// Skip frames until this many wall-clock seconds have passed.
    WaitSeconds(f32),
    /// Unpause game time for a `step_frames` command and remember that it is in flight.
    BeginSteppedFrames,
    /// Pause game time again at the end of a `step_frames` command.
    EndSteppedFrames,
}

fn then_end_frame(step: DriverStep) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    vec![step, DriverStep::EndFrame]
}

// `bevy_picking` reads `WindowEvent`s in `First` of the frame after they are written, so its
// result appears one frame later than the input. One more barrier lets the next command see it.
fn with_picking_barrier(mut steps: Vec<DriverStep>) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    steps.push(DriverStep::EndFrame);
    steps
}

fn bevy_touch_phase(phase: TouchCommandPhase) -> TouchPhase {
    match phase {
        TouchCommandPhase::Started => TouchPhase::Started,
        TouchCommandPhase::Moved => TouchPhase::Moved,
        TouchCommandPhase::Ended => TouchPhase::Ended,
        TouchCommandPhase::Canceled => TouchPhase::Canceled,
    }
}

/// Positions of `frames` evenly spaced moves from `from`, the last exactly at `to`.
fn drag_path(from: Vec2, to: Vec2, frames: u32) -> Vec<Vec2> {
    let frames = frames.max(1);
    (1..=frames)
        .map(|index| {
            if index == frames {
                to
            } else {
                from.lerp(to, index as f32 / frames as f32)
            }
        })
        .collect()
}

fn mouse_drag_steps(from: Vec2, to: Vec2, button: MouseButton, frames: u32) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseMove { position: from }));
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseDown { button }));
    for position in drag_path(from, to, frames) {
        steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseMove { position }));
    }
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseUp { button }));
    with_picking_barrier(steps)
}

fn touch_drag_steps(from: Vec2, to: Vec2, id: u64, frames: u32) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    let touch = |phase, position| DriverStep::Touch {
        phase,
        position,
        id,
    };
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(touch(TouchPhase::Started, from)));
    for position in drag_path(from, to, frames) {
        steps.extend(then_end_frame(touch(TouchPhase::Moved, position)));
    }
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(touch(TouchPhase::Ended, to)));
    with_picking_barrier(steps)
}

fn key_tap_steps(key: KeyCode, text: Option<&str>, hold_frames: u32) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    let mut steps = vec![DriverStep::KeyDown {
        key,
        text: text.map(str::to_owned),
    }];
    steps.extend(repeat_n(DriverStep::EndFrame, hold_frames.max(1) as usize));
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::KeyUp { key }));
    steps
}

fn type_text_steps(text: &str) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    text.chars()
        .flat_map(|character| {
            let key = key_code_for_character(character);
            key_tap_steps(key, Some(&character.to_string()), 1)
        })
        .collect()
}

fn click_steps(button: MouseButton, position: Option<Vec2>) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    let mut steps = Vec::new();
    if let Some(position) = position {
        steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseMove { position }));
    }
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseDown { button }));
    steps.extend(then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseUp { button }));
    with_picking_barrier(steps)
}

/// Expands one command into primitive steps. Every input step is followed by an
/// [`DriverStep::EndFrame`] so game systems see it for at least one frame.
#[must_use]
pub fn expand_command(command: &DriverCommand) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    match command {
        DriverCommand::KeyDown { key, text } => then_end_frame(DriverStep::KeyDown {
            key: *key,
            text: text.clone(),
        }),
        DriverCommand::KeyUp { key } => then_end_frame(DriverStep::KeyUp { key: *key }),
        DriverCommand::KeyTap {
            key,
            text,
            hold_frames,
        } => key_tap_steps(*key, text.as_deref(), *hold_frames),
        DriverCommand::TypeText { text } => type_text_steps(text),
        DriverCommand::MouseMove { x, y } => then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseMove {
            position: Vec2::new(*x, *y),
        }),
        DriverCommand::MouseDown { button } => {
            then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseDown { button: *button })
        }
        DriverCommand::MouseUp { button } => {
            then_end_frame(DriverStep::MouseUp { button: *button })
        }
        DriverCommand::Click { button, x, y } => {
            let position = x.zip(*y).map(|(x, y)| Vec2::new(x, y));
            click_steps(*button, position)
        }
        DriverCommand::Drag {
            from,
            to,
            button,
            frames,
        } => mouse_drag_steps(Vec2::from(*from), Vec2::from(*to), *button, *frames),
        DriverCommand::Scroll { x, y, unit } => {
            with_picking_barrier(then_end_frame(DriverStep::Scroll {
                unit: match unit {
                    ScrollUnit::Line => MouseScrollUnit::Line,
                    ScrollUnit::Pixel => MouseScrollUnit::Pixel,
                },
                x: *x,
                y: *y,
            }))
        }
        DriverCommand::Touch { phase, x, y, id } => {
            with_picking_barrier(then_end_frame(DriverStep::Touch {
                phase: bevy_touch_phase(*phase),
                position: Vec2::new(*x, *y),
                id: *id,
            }))
        }
        DriverCommand::TouchDrag {
            from,
            to,
            id,
            frames,
        } => touch_drag_steps(Vec2::from(*from), Vec2::from(*to), *id, *frames),
        DriverCommand::Pinch { delta } => then_end_frame(DriverStep::Pinch { delta: *delta }),
        DriverCommand::Rotate { delta } => then_end_frame(DriverStep::Rotate { delta: *delta }),
        DriverCommand::Pan { x, y } => then_end_frame(DriverStep::Pan {
            delta: Vec2::new(*x, *y),
        }),
        DriverCommand::DoubleTap {} => then_end_frame(DriverStep::DoubleTap),
        DriverCommand::WaitFrames { frames } => vec![DriverStep::WaitFrames(*frames)],
        DriverCommand::WaitSeconds { seconds } => vec![DriverStep::WaitSeconds(*seconds)],
        DriverCommand::Screenshot { path } => {
            then_end_frame(DriverStep::Screenshot { path: path.clone() })
        }
        DriverCommand::ReleaseAll {} => then_end_frame(DriverStep::ReleaseAll),
        DriverCommand::Quit {} => then_end_frame(DriverStep::Quit),
        DriverCommand::SetSeed { seed } => then_end_frame(DriverStep::SetSeed(*seed)),
        DriverCommand::TimePause {} => time_steps(TimeControlAction::Pause),
        DriverCommand::TimeResume {} => time_steps(TimeControlAction::Resume),
        DriverCommand::TimeSpeed { factor } => time_steps(TimeControlAction::SetSpeed(*factor)),
        DriverCommand::TimeFixedStep { ms } => {
            time_steps(TimeControlAction::FixedStep { milliseconds: *ms })
        }
        DriverCommand::TimeReal {} => time_steps(TimeControlAction::RealTime),
        // Measured live (2026-11-25, 100 ms fixed step): waiting `frames - 1` advanced only
        // `frames - 1` steps, and stepping 1 frame advanced none, so the wait is the full count.
        DriverCommand::StepFrames { frames } => vec![
            DriverStep::BeginSteppedFrames,
            DriverStep::WaitFrames(*frames),
            DriverStep::EndSteppedFrames,
            DriverStep::EndFrame,
        ],
        DriverCommand::SaveState { path } => state_steps(StateOperation::Save, path),
        DriverCommand::LoadState { path } => state_steps(StateOperation::Load, path),
    }
}

fn time_steps(action: TimeControlAction) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    then_end_frame(DriverStep::Time(action))
}

fn state_steps(operation: StateOperation, path: &str) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
    then_end_frame(DriverStep::State {
        operation,
        path: path.to_owned(),
    })
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct PendingWait {
    frames_remaining: u32,
    until: Option<Instant>,
}

/// Steps waiting to run, plus the wait the executor is currently serving.
#[derive(Resource, Debug, Default)]
pub struct DriverQueue {
    steps: VecDeque<DriverStep>,
    wait: PendingWait,
    stepping_frames: bool,
}

impl DriverQueue {
    /// Appends the expansion of every command.
    pub fn enqueue_commands<'a>(&mut self, commands: impl IntoIterator<Item = &'a DriverCommand>) {
        for command in commands {
            self.steps.extend(expand_command(command));
        }
    }

    /// Drops every pending step and wait, then queues a release of everything held.
    ///
    /// When a `step_frames` command has unpaused time and not yet paused it again, the queue also
    /// pauses time again so the cleared command cannot leave the game running.
    pub fn clear_and_release_all(&mut self) {
        let was_stepping_frames = std::mem::take(&mut self.stepping_frames);
        self.steps.clear();
        self.wait = PendingWait::default();
        self.steps.push_back(DriverStep::ReleaseAll);
        if was_stepping_frames {
            self.steps
                .push_back(DriverStep::Time(TimeControlAction::Pause));
        }
        self.steps.push_back(DriverStep::EndFrame);
    }

    /// Records whether a `step_frames` command has unpaused time and not yet paused it again.
    pub fn set_stepping_frames(&mut self, stepping_frames: bool) {
        self.stepping_frames = stepping_frames;
    }

    /// Number of steps not yet run.
    #[must_use]
    pub fn step_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.steps.len()
    }

    /// True when no step is pending and no wait is active.
    #[must_use]
    pub fn is_idle(&self) -> bool {
        self.steps.is_empty() && self.wait.frames_remaining == 0 && self.wait.until.is_none()
    }

    /// Removes and returns the next step.
    pub fn pop_next_step(&mut self) -> Option<DriverStep> {
        self.steps.pop_front()
    }

    /// Starts skipping frames. Returns false when `frames` is zero and no wait is needed.
    ///
    /// The frame that calls this counts as the first waited frame.
    pub fn begin_frame_wait(&mut self, frames: u32) -> bool {
        self.wait.frames_remaining = frames.saturating_sub(1);
        frames > 0
    }

    /// Starts waiting `seconds` of wall-clock time from `now`. Returns false when `seconds` is
    /// zero, negative, or too large to represent, and no wait is needed.
    pub fn begin_timed_wait(&mut self, now: Instant, seconds: f32) -> bool {
        if seconds <= 0.0 {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(until) = Duration::try_from_secs_f32(seconds)
            .ok()
            .and_then(|length| now.checked_add(length))
        else {
            return false;
        };
        self.wait.until = Some(until);
        true
    }

    /// Advances an active wait by one frame and reports whether the executor must stay idle.
    pub fn is_still_waiting(&mut self, now: Instant) -> bool {
        if self.wait.frames_remaining > 0 {
            self.wait.frames_remaining -= 1;
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(until) = self.wait.until {
            if now < until {
                return true;
            }
            self.wait.until = None;
        }
        false
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::commands::parse_command_text;

    fn expand(text: &str) -> Vec<DriverStep> {
        expand_command(&parse_command_text(text).expect("command should parse"))
    }

    #[test]
    fn key_tap_has_down_barriers_up_barrier() {
        let steps = expand(r#"{"cmd":"key_tap","key":"KeyA","hold_frames":3}"#);
        assert_eq!(
            steps,
            vec![
                DriverStep::KeyDown {
                    key: KeyCode::KeyA,
                    text: None
                },
                DriverStep::EndFrame,
                DriverStep::EndFrame,
                DriverStep::EndFrame,
                DriverStep::KeyUp { key: KeyCode::KeyA },
                DriverStep::EndFrame,
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn drag_moves_evenly_and_ends_exactly_at_target() {
        let steps = expand(r#"{"cmd":"drag","from":[0,0],"to":[100,50],"frames":4}"#);
        let moves: Vec<Vec2> = steps
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|step| match step {
                DriverStep::MouseMove { position } => Some(*position),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(moves.len(), 5);
        assert_eq!(moves[0], Vec2::ZERO);
        assert_eq!(moves[1], Vec2::new(25.0, 12.5));
        assert_eq!(moves[4], Vec2::new(100.0, 50.0));
        let down = steps
            .iter()
            .position(|step| matches!(step, DriverStep::MouseDown { .. }));
        let up = steps
            .iter()
            .position(|step| matches!(step, DriverStep::MouseUp { .. }));
        assert!(down < up);
        assert_eq!(steps.last(), Some(&DriverStep::EndFrame));
    }

    #[test]
    fn touch_drag_starts_moves_and_ends() {
        let steps = expand(r#"{"cmd":"touch_drag","from":[1,1],"to":[3,3],"frames":2,"id":7}"#);
        let phases: Vec<TouchPhase> = steps
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|step| match step {
                DriverStep::Touch { phase, .. } => Some(*phase),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            phases,
            vec![
                TouchPhase::Started,
                TouchPhase::Moved,
                TouchPhase::Moved,
                TouchPhase::Ended
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn click_with_position_moves_first() {
        let steps = expand(r#"{"cmd":"click","x":5,"y":6}"#);
        assert_eq!(
            steps.first(),
            Some(&DriverStep::MouseMove {
                position: Vec2::new(5.0, 6.0)
            })
        );
        assert_eq!(steps.len(), 7);
    }

    #[test]
    fn type_text_maps_characters_to_keys_one_frame_each() {
        let steps = expand(r#"{"cmd":"type_text","text":"Ab 1\n!"}"#);
        let downs: Vec<(KeyCode, Option<String>)> = steps
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|step| match step {
                DriverStep::KeyDown { key, text } => Some((*key, text.clone())),
                _ => None,
            })
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(
            downs,
            vec![
                (KeyCode::KeyA, Some("A".to_owned())),
                (KeyCode::KeyB, Some("b".to_owned())),
                (KeyCode::Space, Some(" ".to_owned())),
                (KeyCode::Digit1, Some("1".to_owned())),
                (KeyCode::Enter, Some("\n".to_owned())),
                (
                    KeyCode::Unidentified(NativeKeyCode::Unidentified),
                    Some("!".to_owned())
                ),
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(steps.len(), downs.len() * 4);
    }

    #[test]
    fn step_frames_resumes_waits_then_pauses() {
        assert_eq!(
            expand(r#"{"cmd":"step_frames","frames":3}"#),
            vec![
                DriverStep::BeginSteppedFrames,
                DriverStep::WaitFrames(3),
                DriverStep::EndSteppedFrames,
                DriverStep::EndFrame,
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn seed_time_and_state_commands_end_with_a_barrier() {
        assert_eq!(
            expand(r#"{"cmd":"set_seed","seed":9}"#),
            vec![DriverStep::SetSeed(9), DriverStep::EndFrame]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            expand(r#"{"cmd":"time_fixed_step","ms":20}"#),
            vec![
                DriverStep::Time(TimeControlAction::FixedStep { milliseconds: 20.0 }),
                DriverStep::EndFrame
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            expand(r#"{"cmd":"time_speed","factor":2}"#),
            vec![
                DriverStep::Time(TimeControlAction::SetSpeed(2.0)),
                DriverStep::EndFrame
            ]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            expand(r#"{"cmd":"load_state","path":"slot.txt"}"#),
            vec![
                DriverStep::State {
                    operation: StateOperation::Load,
                    path: "slot.txt".to_owned()
                },
                DriverStep::EndFrame
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn character_tables_round_trip() {
        for (character, key) in CHARACTER_KEYS {
            assert_eq!(key_code_for_character(character), key);
            assert_eq!(character_for_key_code(key), Some(character));
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn frame_wait_lasts_the_requested_number_of_frames() {
        let mut queue = DriverQueue::default();
        let now = Instant::now();
        assert!(queue.begin_frame_wait(3));
        assert!(queue.is_still_waiting(now));
        assert!(queue.is_still_waiting(now));
        assert!(!queue.is_still_waiting(now));
    }

    #[test]
    fn timed_wait_ends_once_the_wall_clock_passes_the_deadline() {
        let mut queue = DriverQueue::default();
        let start = Instant::now();
        assert!(queue.begin_timed_wait(start, 2.0));
        assert!(!queue.is_idle());
        assert!(queue.is_still_waiting(start + Duration::from_secs(1)));
        assert!(!queue.is_still_waiting(start + Duration::from_secs(3)));
        assert!(queue.is_idle());
        assert!(!queue.begin_timed_wait(start, 0.0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn clearing_during_step_frames_queues_a_pause() {
        let mut queue = DriverQueue::default();
        queue.set_stepping_frames(true);
        queue.clear_and_release_all();
        assert_eq!(
            Vec::from(queue.steps.clone()),
            vec![
                DriverStep::ReleaseAll,
                DriverStep::Time(TimeControlAction::Pause),
                DriverStep::EndFrame
            ]
        );
        queue.clear_and_release_all();
        assert_eq!(queue.step_count(), 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn pointer_commands_end_with_two_barriers() {
        for text in [
            r#"{"cmd":"click"}"#,
            r#"{"cmd":"scroll","y":1}"#,
            r#"{"cmd":"touch","phase":"ended","x":1,"y":1}"#,
        ] {
            let steps = expand(text);
            let tail = &steps[steps.len() - 2..];
            assert_eq!(tail, [DriverStep::EndFrame, DriverStep::EndFrame], "{text}");
        }
    }
}

```

`src/time_control.rs`:

```rs
//! Pausing, slowing, and single-stepping game time.

use std::{
    fmt,
    time::{Duration, TryFromFloatSecsError},
};

use bevy::{
    prelude::*,
    time::{Time, TimeUpdateStrategy, Virtual},
};

use crate::status::{DriverStatus, record_error_in_world};

// Bevy's own default, which `Time<Virtual>` does not expose publicly.
const DEFAULT_VIRTUAL_MAX_DELTA: Duration = Duration::from_millis(250);

/// One change to the game clock, applied by a world command.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum TimeControlAction {
    /// Stop `Time<Virtual>`.
    Pause,
    /// Let `Time<Virtual>` run again.
    Resume,
    /// Set the relative speed of `Time<Virtual>`.
    SetSpeed(f32),
    /// Make every frame advance real time by exactly this many milliseconds.
    FixedStep {
        /// Milliseconds per frame.
        milliseconds: f64,
    },
    /// Return to automatic real-time updates and speed 1.0, without changing the pause state.
    RealTime,
}

/// Why a [`TimeControlAction`] could not be applied.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum TimeControlError {
    /// A time resource is missing, so `TimePlugin` is not part of the app.
    MissingResource(&'static str),
    /// The speed is not a finite number above zero.
    InvalidSpeed(f32),
    /// The step cannot be represented as a duration.
    InvalidFixedStep(TryFromFloatSecsError),
}

impl fmt::Display for TimeControlError {
    fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        match self {
            Self::MissingResource(name) => write!(formatter, "time control needs {name}"),
            Self::InvalidSpeed(factor) => {
                write!(
                    formatter,
                    "time speed {factor} is not a finite number above 0"
                )
            }
            Self::InvalidFixedStep(source) => write!(formatter, "invalid fixed step: {source}"),
        }
    }
}

impl std::error::Error for TimeControlError {}

fn virtual_time(world: &mut World) -> Result<Mut<'_, Time<Virtual>>, TimeControlError> {
    world
        .get_resource_mut::<Time<Virtual>>()
        .ok_or(TimeControlError::MissingResource("Time<Virtual>"))
}

fn update_strategy(world: &mut World) -> Result<Mut<'_, TimeUpdateStrategy>, TimeControlError> {
    world
        .get_resource_mut::<TimeUpdateStrategy>()
        .ok_or(TimeControlError::MissingResource("TimeUpdateStrategy"))
}

/// Applies one clock change directly to the world.
///
/// # Errors
///
/// Returns [`TimeControlError`] when a time resource is missing or the value is unusable.
pub fn apply_time_action(
    world: &mut World,
    action: TimeControlAction,
) -> Result<(), TimeControlError> {
    match action {
        TimeControlAction::Pause => virtual_time(world)?.pause(),
        TimeControlAction::Resume => virtual_time(world)?.unpause(),
        TimeControlAction::SetSpeed(factor) => {
            if !factor.is_finite() || factor <= 0.0 {
                return Err(TimeControlError::InvalidSpeed(factor));
            }
            virtual_time(world)?.set_relative_speed(factor);
        }
        TimeControlAction::FixedStep { milliseconds } => {
            let step = Duration::try_from_secs_f64(milliseconds / 1000.0)
                .map_err(TimeControlError::InvalidFixedStep)?;
            *update_strategy(world)? = TimeUpdateStrategy::ManualDuration(step);
            // Otherwise `Time<Virtual>` clamps the step and the game advances less than asked.
            let mut virtual_time = virtual_time(world)?;
            if step > virtual_time.max_delta() {
                virtual_time.set_max_delta(step);
            }
        }
        TimeControlAction::RealTime => {
            *update_strategy(world)? = TimeUpdateStrategy::Automatic;
            let mut virtual_time = virtual_time(world)?;
            virtual_time.set_relative_speed(1.0);
            virtual_time.set_max_delta(DEFAULT_VIRTUAL_MAX_DELTA);
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Queues a world command that applies `action`, recording any failure in [`DriverStatus`].
pub fn queue_time_action(commands: &mut Commands, action: TimeControlAction) {
    commands.queue(move |world: &mut World| {
        if let Err(problem) = apply_time_action(world, action) {
            record_error_in_world(world, problem.to_string());
        }
    });
}

/// Registers the system that reports the game clock in the status.
pub struct DriverTimeControlPlugin;

impl Plugin for DriverTimeControlPlugin {
    fn build(&self, app: &mut App) {
        app.add_systems(Last, report_time_state);
    }
}

fn report_time_state(
    virtual_time: Res<Time<Virtual>>,
    update_strategy: Res<TimeUpdateStrategy>,
    mut status: ResMut<DriverStatus>,
) {
    status.paused = virtual_time.is_paused();
    status.speed = virtual_time.relative_speed();
    status.fixed_step_ms = match *update_strategy {
        TimeUpdateStrategy::ManualDuration(step) => Some(step.as_secs_f64() * 1000.0),
        _ => None,
    };
    status.virtual_elapsed_seconds = virtual_time.elapsed_secs_f64();
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn world_with_time() -> World {
        let mut world = World::new();
        world.init_resource::<Time<Virtual>>();
        world.init_resource::<TimeUpdateStrategy>();
        world
    }

    #[test]
    fn pause_speed_and_fixed_step_change_the_clock() {
        let mut world = world_with_time();
        apply_time_action(&mut world, TimeControlAction::Pause).expect("pause");
        apply_time_action(&mut world, TimeControlAction::SetSpeed(2.0)).expect("speed");
        let fixed_step = TimeControlAction::FixedStep { milliseconds: 20.0 };
        apply_time_action(&mut world, fixed_step).expect("fixed step");
        let virtual_time = world.resource::<Time<Virtual>>();
        assert!(virtual_time.is_paused());
        assert!((virtual_time.relative_speed() - 2.0).abs() < f32::EPSILON);
        assert!(matches!(
            world.resource::<TimeUpdateStrategy>(),
            TimeUpdateStrategy::ManualDuration(step) if *step == Duration::from_millis(20)
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn real_time_restores_speed_and_strategy_but_not_the_pause() {
        let mut world = world_with_time();
        apply_time_action(&mut world, TimeControlAction::Pause).expect("pause");
        apply_time_action(&mut world, TimeControlAction::SetSpeed(3.0)).expect("speed");
        let fixed_step = TimeControlAction::FixedStep { milliseconds: 5.0 };
        apply_time_action(&mut world, fixed_step).expect("fixed step");
        apply_time_action(&mut world, TimeControlAction::RealTime).expect("real time");
        let virtual_time = world.resource::<Time<Virtual>>();
        assert!(virtual_time.is_paused());
        assert!((virtual_time.relative_speed() - 1.0).abs() < f32::EPSILON);
        assert!(matches!(
            world.resource::<TimeUpdateStrategy>(),
            TimeUpdateStrategy::Automatic
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn large_fixed_step_raises_max_delta_and_real_time_restores_it() {
        let mut world = world_with_time();
        let fixed_step = TimeControlAction::FixedStep {
            milliseconds: 500.0,
        };
        apply_time_action(&mut world, fixed_step).expect("fixed step");
        let max_delta = world.resource::<Time<Virtual>>().max_delta();
        assert_eq!(max_delta, Duration::from_millis(500));
        apply_time_action(&mut world, TimeControlAction::RealTime).expect("real time");
        let max_delta = world.resource::<Time<Virtual>>().max_delta();
        assert_eq!(max_delta, DEFAULT_VIRTUAL_MAX_DELTA);
    }

    #[test]
    fn rejects_unusable_values_and_missing_resources() {
        let mut world = world_with_time();
        assert!(apply_time_action(&mut world, TimeControlAction::SetSpeed(0.0)).is_err());
        assert!(
            apply_time_action(
                &mut world,
                TimeControlAction::FixedStep {
                    milliseconds: f64::NAN
                }
            )
            .is_err()
        );
        let mut empty_world = World::new();
        assert!(apply_time_action(&mut empty_world, TimeControlAction::Pause).is_err());
    }
}

```

`tools/drive.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Command line helper for the bevy_input_driver BRP methods (Python 3 standard library only)."""

import argparse
import json
import sys
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

EXIT_TIMEOUT = 1
EXIT_UNREACHABLE = 2
EXIT_BRP_ERROR = 3
POLL_SECONDS = 0.1


class DriverUnreachable(Exception):
    """Raised when the game does not answer on the chosen port."""


def call(port, method, params=None):
    """Sends one JSON-RPC 2.0 request and returns the `result`, or exits 3 on a BRP error."""
    body = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": method}
    if params is not None:
        body["params"] = params
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/",
        data=json.dumps(body).encode("utf-8"),
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
        method="POST",
    )
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=10) as response:
            reply = json.load(response)
    except (urllib.error.URLError, ConnectionError, TimeoutError) as problem:
        raise DriverUnreachable(
            f"cannot reach the game on 127.0.0.1:{port} ({problem}). "
            "Is it running with the input driver enabled?"
        ) from problem
    if "error" in reply:
        print(json.dumps(reply["error"], indent=2), file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(EXIT_BRP_ERROR)
    return reply.get("result")


def read_commands_from_file(path):
    """Reads a JSON Lines file, skipping blank lines and lines starting with // or #."""
    commands = []
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        for number, raw_line in enumerate(handle, start=1):
            line = raw_line.strip()
            if not line or line.startswith("//") or line.startswith("#"):
                continue
            try:
                commands.append(json.loads(line))
            except json.JSONDecodeError as problem:
                print(f"{path} line {number}: {problem}", file=sys.stderr)
                sys.exit(EXIT_TIMEOUT)
    return commands


def wait_until_idle(port, timeout, game_was_reachable=False):
    """Polls drive/status until idle. Returns the final status, or None on timeout.

    Once the game has answered at least once (here or in the call that sent the commands), a
    later loss of the connection means it exited, for example after a quit command. That prints
    {"game_exited": true} and exits 0.
    """
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while True:
        try:
            status = call(port, "drive/status")
        except DriverUnreachable:
            if not game_was_reachable:
                raise
            print(json.dumps({"game_exited": True}))
            sys.exit(0)
        game_was_reachable = True
        if status.get("idle"):
            return status
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            print(json.dumps(status, indent=2))
            print(f"timed out after {timeout} seconds before the driver went idle", file=sys.stderr)
            return None
        time.sleep(POLL_SECONDS)


def send_commands(args, commands):
    """Sends commands as one array, then optionally waits."""
    print(json.dumps(call(args.port, "drive/send", commands), indent=2))
    if args.wait:
        finish_wait(args, game_was_reachable=True)


def finish_wait(args, game_was_reachable=False):
    """Waits for idle and exits 1 on timeout."""
    status = wait_until_idle(args.port, args.timeout, game_was_reachable)
    if status is None:
        sys.exit(EXIT_TIMEOUT)
    print(json.dumps(status, indent=2))


def build_parser():
    """Builds the argument parser."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Drive a Bevy game running bevy_input_driver.",
        epilog=(
            "exit codes: 0 success (also when the game exited while waiting, after printing "
            '{"game_exited": true}), 1 timeout or bad input, 2 game unreachable at start, '
            "3 error reply from the game"
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=15702)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="action", required=True)

    send = commands.add_parser("send", help="send JSON commands")
    send.add_argument("commands", nargs="+", help="a JSON command object or array")
    send.add_argument("--wait", action="store_true", help="wait until idle afterward")
    send.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=30.0)

    send_file = commands.add_parser("send-file", help="send a JSON Lines file")
    send_file.add_argument("path")
    send_file.add_argument("--wait", action="store_true", help="wait until idle afterward")
    send_file.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=30.0)

    commands.add_parser("status", help="print driver status")
    commands.add_parser("clear", help="empty the queue and release held input")

    wait = commands.add_parser("wait", help="wait until the driver is idle")
    wait.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=30.0)
    return parser


def collect_send_commands(raw_arguments):
    """Flattens each argument, an object or an array, into one command list."""
    commands = []
    for raw in raw_arguments:
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(raw)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as problem:
            print(f"invalid JSON argument {raw!r}: {problem}", file=sys.stderr)
            sys.exit(EXIT_TIMEOUT)
        commands.extend(parsed if isinstance(parsed, list) else [parsed])
    return commands


def run(args):
    """Runs the chosen action."""
    if args.action == "send":
        send_commands(args, collect_send_commands(args.commands))
    elif args.action == "send-file":
        send_commands(args, read_commands_from_file(args.path))
    elif args.action == "status":
        print(json.dumps(call(args.port, "drive/status"), indent=2))
    elif args.action == "clear":
        print(json.dumps(call(args.port, "drive/clear"), indent=2))
    elif args.action == "wait":
        finish_wait(args)


def main():
    """Entry point."""
    args = build_parser().parse_args()
    try:
        run(args)
    except DriverUnreachable as problem:
        print(str(problem), file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(EXIT_UNREACHABLE)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```
